# Dependency map — 8 areas, menu -> admission -> lobby -> trials -> results -> rematch

Produced by 8 parallel read-only agents, 31.08.2026. Max 4 players throughout.

## Kenopsia_DEV main-menu place (placeId 129909297895850), source C:/Users/Asus/Claude/Kenopsia_Roblox Project/dev-src/ — 21 files, all read. Cross-checked against studio-src/ (MainGame, 110672791536316) for the return path and DataStore keys.

The menu place is a self-contained, in-memory party lobby with no persistence and no cross-place messaging. RoomService owns parties for the lifetime of one menu server; PartyRegistry (the MemoryStore code) is present in the place tree but is never required by anything and is completely dead. The only exit to MainGame is PlaylistService.teleportRoom: RoomService fires RunRequested after a 5-second countdown, PlaylistService reserves a server on 110672791536316 and calls TeleportAsync with the whole live roster. TeleportData is never set — the TeleportOptions object carries only ReservedServerAccessCode, so MainGame receives no roster, no room id, no host and no trial selection; membership is implied purely by who arrives. A lone player cannot press play: three independent server gates and the wall UI all enforce Config.Room.MinPlayers = 2, and the shipped dev client never fires RoomStartRequest, RoomQuickJoinRequest or TrialPracticeRequest at all (there are exactly two FireServer call sites in the entire dev client). The menu place touches no DataStore whatsoever — MainGame's KenopsiaProfile_v1 / key "u_%d" is written only in studio-src, so nothing a player does in the menu survives the session. There is no handshake in either direction: no MessagingService in dev-src, no GetJoinData anywhere, and SoloExit sends a lone MainGame survivor back with a bare TeleportAsync(MENU_PLACE_ID, {plr}) carrying no options and no data. A returning player is therefore indistinguishable from a fresh joiner and sees the ordinary landing screen with no explanation. Ping-pong is impossible from the menu side: the return leg is automatic but the outbound leg requires two players who have both pressed READY, and the READY row is disabled below two.

### Unfinished / dormant

- **TeleportData is never populated on the menu -> MainGame hop. MainGame gets a reserved server and a group of arriving players, but no roster, no room id, no host id, no join policy, no trial selection and no provenance.** — intentional: unclear — no comment either way. The comment at PlaylistService.luau:145-146 ('reserve a private server on the main place and move the whole lobby there together') suggests the reserved server IS the intended party channel, i.e. deliberate minimalism. But MainGame then has no way to distinguish a menu-formed party from strangers, and no way to know who the host was. This is the single most load-bearing gap in the menu->game contract. — [SOURCE] dev-src/.../PlaylistService.luau:167-169 is the whole options setup: `local opts = Instance.new("TeleportOptions")` / `opts.ReservedServerAccessCode = code` / `TeleportService:TeleportAsync(MAIN_PLACE_ID, players, opts)`. Repo-wide grep for SetTeleportData/TeleportData/GetJoinData across studio-src/ and dev-src/ = 0 hits.
- **PartyRegistry is a complete, typed, MemoryStore-backed cross-server code registry that nothing calls. Its claim/lookup/release/touch API and the KenopsiaRooms_v1 hash map are entirely unreachable.** — intentional: yes — RoomService.luau:2-4 states it: `-- PartyRegistry intentionally remains dormant: browsing, codes and approval requests live only for the lifetime of this menu server.` Documented dead code kept for a future cross-server lobby. Consequence: party codes work only within one menu server instance, which is invisible to a player who shares a code with a friend on a different server. — [SOURCE] dev-src/.../PartyRegistry.luau:37/79/101/111 define the four public functions; Main.server.luau:5-7 requires only RoomService/PlaylistService/MenuPresenceService; grep of dev-src for 'PartyRegistry' finds no require anywhere.
- **Three server-side remote handlers have no client caller in the shipped dev client: RoomStartRequest (host force-start), RoomQuickJoinRequest (auto-join best public), and TrialPracticeRequest (host-only practice run).** — intentional: partly — RoomService.luau:516-517 explains quickJoin as `-- Backwards-compatible route for any older client still present during a rolling publish.` PlaylistService.luau:207 labels the practice path `-- TRIAL:`. RoomStartRequest has no such note and looks like an orphan from the pre-split menu. All three remain live attack surface for an exploiter. — [SOURCE] Handlers at RoomService.luau:746-751 (quickJoin), :767-772 (startRun) and PlaylistService.luau:209-220 (practice). The entire dev client contains exactly two FireServer sites: SessionWallController.luau:139 (which fires 8 named remotes, none of these three) and TrialController.luau:48 (TrialInput).
- **room.practiceRun is set before the countdown but is only cleared when the run actually fires. abortCountdown does not clear it, so an aborted practice countdown leaves the flag set on the room.** — intentional: no — a stale flag inverts the routing decision at PlaylistService.luau:225-230. A room that aborted a practice countdown and then legitimately starts with two ready players runs the in-menu practice playlist instead of teleporting to MainGame. Requires the practice remote to be reachable, which today it is only for an exploiter, so it is latent rather than live. — [SOURCE] PlaylistService.luau:218 `room.practiceRun = true` then :219 forceStart. RoomService.luau:254-268 abortCountdown resets token/phase/countdown and nothing else. The only clear is PlaylistService.luau:226 `room.practiceRun = nil`, inside the RunRequested branch.
- **dev-src has no TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed handler. If TeleportAsync succeeds as a call but a given player's teleport init fails, that player stays in the menu inside a room whose phase is permanently 'Playing'.** — intentional: no — the failure path was written for the synchronous case only. The player is left unfrozen, walking the menu street, staring at AWAITING PROGRAM, with no route out except rejoining. — [SOURCE] PlaylistService.luau:165-177 pcalls the teleport and only handles the synchronous error path; grep for TeleportInitFailed in dev-src = 0 hits (the only handler in the repo is studio-src/.../SoloExit.luau:158). MenuPresenceService.luau:55 `return room.phase ~= "Playing"` then releases that player's character, and MenuController.luau:243 leaves the monitor reading `title.Text = "AWAITING PROGRAM..."`.
- **MainGame's EmoteService documents and subscribes to a cross-place grant channel whose DEV-side publisher does not exist.** — intentional: no — the comment asserts a DEV behaviour ('a crate ceremony in DEV publishes here') that is not implemented in dev-src. Either the crate ceremony was planned for the menu place and never built, or it lives somewhere not mirrored in this repo. Today the subscription can only ever receive MainGame's own publishes. — [SOURCE] studio-src/.../EmoteService.luau:471-473 `-- The CROSS-PLACE path. DEV and MainGame share universe 10640788131 and the` / `-- KenopsiaProfile_v1 DataStore, so a crate ceremony in DEV publishes here and` / `-- the player sees the emote without a rejoin.`; SubscribeAsync at :479. grep of dev-src for MessagingService = 0 hits, and for DataStore/KenopsiaProfile = 0 hits.
- **teleportRoom has no lower bound on party size. It rejects only an empty roster, so a room that drops to one live player between the countdown finishing and the teleport running will send that single player into a fresh reserved MainGame server.** — intentional: no — the guard was written against zero, not against one. The landing state is the bad part: a lone arrival in a RESERVED server cannot be joined by matchmaking, and studio-src/.../SoloExit.luau:70 requires `hadCompanyFlag == true`, which never becomes true on a server that only ever held one player. That player is stuck with no exit. — [SOURCE] PlaylistService.luau:154-157 `if #players == 0 then RoomServiceRef.finishRun(room) return end` is the only size check. RoomService.luau:255-257 abortCountdown returns early once phase is no longer 'Starting', so a member lost after phase flips to 'Playing' aborts nothing.

### Risks

- PING-PONG: NOT POSSIBLE from the menu side, and here is the code that decides it. Three independent server gates all read Config.Room.MinPlayers = 2 (GameConfig.luau:19): RoomService.luau:311 `if Config.Room.RequireAllReady and room.phase == "Waiting" and #room.members >= minPlayers() and allReady(room) then` (the auto-start), RoomService.luau:598-599 `if ready and #room.members < minPlayers() then fail(player, string.format("MINIMUM %d PLAYERS", minPlayers()))` (a lone player cannot even mark himself ready), and RoomService.luau:625-627 `if #room.members < minPlayers() then fail(player, "NOT ENOUGH PLAYERS")` (force-start). The UI blocks it a fourth time at PartyWallView.luau:943 `local canReady = count >= minPlayers and room.phase ~= "Playing"`. The one start path with NO count check is RoomService.luau:316-318 forceStart, but its only caller sets room.practiceRun first (PlaylistService.luau:218) and PlaylistService.luau:225-227 routes that to the LOCAL playlist, never to teleportRoom. So a lone returning player physically cannot re-teleport himself to MainGame, automatically or manually.
- The return leg is automatic while the outbound leg is manual and needs a second human. A player SoloExit'd back arrives in a PUBLIC menu server (SoloExit.luau:104 passes no TeleportOptions) with an empty party and must find a stranger or a friend to play again. That is the intended design, but it means the menu place is where a solo player permanently comes to rest.
- A lone arrival in MainGame is a dead end with no exit. SoloExit.luau:70 `return hadCompanyFlag == true and remainingCount == 1` — hadCompany only becomes true once the server has actually held two players (SoloExit.luau:116-118, :135-138). A reserved MainGame server that only ever receives one player never sets it, so that player is never sent back. Reachable via the teleportRoom race (PlaylistService.luau:154, no >=2 check) or if the second player's teleport fails during loading.
- Every lobby remote is reachable by any client, and three of them have no legitimate caller. An exploiter in the menu place can fire TrialPracticeRequest (PlaylistService.luau:209) and, as host of a one-man room, reach forceStart -> beginCountdown with no member-count check at all — running the practice playlist solo. Rate limits are per-second only (PlaylistService.luau:210 `allow(player, 2)`), not per-session.
- Party codes are per-server only. RoomService.luau:19 `local byCode = {}` is a plain table in one server's memory, and PartyRegistry (the cross-server registry) is dormant. Two friends who join the menu place at the same time land in different public menu servers roughly as often as not, and the code one gives the other will return 'UNKNOWN CODE' (RoomService.luau:418) with no hint that the reason is server sharding.
- The menu place persists nothing. Theme.luau:120 `-- Session settings. No DataStore in the MVP by design, so these reset on leave.` Text scale, retro filter, scanlines, reduce-effects, reduce-flicker, animations, cursor, UI sound and music (MenuController.luau:64-74) are all rebuilt at defaults every join — including every SoloExit return. A player bounced back from MainGame loses every accessibility setting they had chosen.
- The whole menu depends on live-only objects that no Rojo file can guarantee: PlayerGui.KenopsiaGui (Main.client.luau:14, hard-indexed at MenuController.luau:54 `gui.Monitor.ScreenArea`), workspace.MenuStage.CharacterAnchor (MenuPresenceService.luau:36-37) and workspace.MenuStage.SessionDisplay (SessionWallController.luau:101-102). kenopsia-dev.project.json maps only ReplicatedStorage/Config, ServerScriptService and StarterPlayer — no StarterGui and no Workspace. A publish that loses any of them yields a menu with no wall, or an unfrozen character wandering the street.
- MenuPresenceService's reconcile loop (MenuPresenceService.luau:211-225, 1 Hz, forever) is the only thing that re-freezes a character after finishRun, because finishRun fires no event (comment at :206-210). If it ever errors out of its `while true` the freeze silently stops healing for the rest of the server's life, with no log.
- TeleportAsync is called on the whole party in one call (PlaylistService.luau:169) with no retry. The pcall at :165-170 catches only synchronous throws; on failure every member gets 'TRANSPORT FAILED - TRY AGAIN' and the room resets to Waiting with all ready flags cleared (RoomService.luau:648-652), so the party must re-ready from scratch.

### Uncertainties

- NEEDS_RUNTIME — whether the LIVE Kenopsia_DEV place (129909297895850) actually runs the code in dev-src/. The known hazard was stated for studio-src, but the same drift risk applies here and I could not check it: this was a read-only pass with no Studio session on the menu place. Settling observation: connect Studio to place 129909297895850 and diff ServerScriptService.KenopsiaServer.Services.PlaylistService against dev-src/.../PlaylistService.luau — specifically whether line 8 still reads `local MAIN_PLACE_ID = 110672791536316` and whether lines 167-169 still set only ReservedServerAccessCode.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME — whether the live menu client fires any remote that dev-src does not. My 'a lone player cannot start' conclusion rests partly on the client never firing RoomStartRequest or TrialPracticeRequest. The server gates hold regardless for RoomStartRequest (count-checked at RoomService.luau:625), but TrialPracticeRequest reaches forceStart with NO count check. Settling observation: script_grep the live dev place for `FireServer` and confirm the only hits are SessionWallController:139 and TrialController:48.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME — the existence and exact shape of PlayerGui.KenopsiaGui, workspace.MenuStage.CharacterAnchor and workspace.MenuStage.SessionDisplay in the live menu place. None are in kenopsia-dev.project.json. Everything I say about what a returning player SEES is inferred from code paths, not observed. Settling observation: inspect_instance on workspace.MenuStage and on a live player's PlayerGui, and confirm KenopsiaGui.Monitor.ScreenArea.Content.Screens exists with children named Countdown/TrialIntro/Trial/TrialResult/FinalResult.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME — nobody has ever observed the menu -> MainGame teleport succeed. It cannot fire in Studio at all (PlaylistService.luau:158-164 short-circuits with 'TELEPORT UNAVAILABLE IN STUDIO'), and the return leg carries the same debt in writing: docs/QA/2026-08-31-solo-exit.md:120-125 `## Still needs production (test debt)` / `the teleport itself has only been verified up to the call`. Settling observation: a published two-player run — menu console shows no '[PlaylistService] teleport failed', both clients land in one reserved MainGame server, then one leaves and the MainGame console prints '[SoloExit] <name> left - <name> is alone, returning to the main menu' followed by that client arriving back in the menu.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME — how wide the teleportRoom lone-arrival race actually is. RoomService.luau:297 fires RunRequested synchronously and PlaylistService.luau:229 defers with task.spawn, so the roster snapshot at PlaylistService.luau:148-152 is taken one scheduler resume later; I cannot size that window from source, nor can I tell how often a player's teleport init fails after a successful TeleportAsync. Settling observation: in a published 2-player run, have one player force-quit during the 5-second countdown and again immediately after it hits zero, and check whether the MainGame server ever boots with exactly one player.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME — whether anything outside this repo publishes to MessagingService topic KenopsiaGrant_v1 from the DEV place (EmoteService.luau:471-473 asserts a 'crate ceremony in DEV' that has no code in dev-src). Settling observation: script_search the live dev place for 'MessagingService'; if it returns nothing, the comment in studio-src is stale and should be corrected.
- NOT CHECKED — I did not read the MainGame side beyond SoloExit, Profiles.start/keys and EmoteService's messaging block. What the arriving party actually sees in MainGame (Lobby.luau, studio-src RoomService's own MinPlayers=2 flow, MachineFlow) is outside this area and I make no claim about it.

## persistence (Profiles / Badges / ProgressionRules / DataStore + MemoryStore use across both places)

All progression persistence in Kenopsia lives in exactly one module: studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/Profiles.luau, which owns the single DataStore "KenopsiaProfile_v1" keyed "u_%d" (userId). [RUNTIME] I confirmed against the connected live MainGame Studio (placeId 110672791536316): across all 115 scripts in the place, GetDataStore appears only in Profiles (lines 621, 625), and the two UpdateAsync call sites are Profiles:110 (load) and Profiles:132 (save) — there is no SetAsync anywhere. The design is volatile-first: every joiner immediately gets an in-memory ProgressionRules.defaultProfile() and play only ever touches that table; the DataStore is best-effort background traffic. The session lock is `_lock = { jobId = game.JobId, ts = os.time() }` — jobId plus timestamp, with NO per-load nonce/GUID — stealable after 180 s. The critical structural gap: trySave never inspects the stored `_lock` before overwriting, and there is no per-player queue, mutex or in-flight flag, so several code paths can have two UpdateAsync calls in flight on the same key at once. Writes happen at four places (loader "left while loading" branch, PlayerRemoving, the fire-and-forget flush thread, BindToClose); flush is called from the verdict loop (MachineFlow:1325) and from two EmoteService grant paths. A slow load is destructive: when the stored profile lands it replaces the live table wholesale (`entry.data = data`, Profiles:244), so anything earned during the load window is discarded. A "cannot save" state is computed and put on the wire (the `volatile` field in the profilestate and shiftreport packets) but [RUNTIME] nothing in the live place renders it: 0 matches for "profilestate" across the 28 StarterPlayer scripts, 0 matches for "OFFLINE" across all 115 scripts, and the sole shiftreport consumer (SocialClient:78) reads only openSeats. The DEV/menu place (129909297895850, dev-src/) has zero DataStore code — it only uses a MemoryStore HashMap "KenopsiaRooms_v1" for 6-char party codes — so no progression is read or written there at all.

### Nodes

| id | kind | file | role |
|---|---|---|---|
| Profiles | module | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/Profiles.luau` | The ONLY DataStore owner in the game. Volatile-first in-memory profile per userId in `live`; session lock, migrate, merge-write, three flush moments, BindToClose. 663 lines; [RUNTIME] live copy is also 663 lines and byte-identical in the lock/save/flush regions I sampled. |
| KenopsiaProfile_v1 | datastore | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/Profiles.luau:39` | The one persistent store. Key format "u_%d" (Profiles:40) => key `u_<UserId>`. Value = the profile table plus a non-owned `_lock` field. |
| ProgressionRules | module | `studio-src/ReplicatedStorage/Kenopsia/Shared/Rules/ProgressionRules.luau` | Pure rules: defaultProfile (schema v1 shape), sessionXp, clearanceFor, tickStreak, rollQuests/applyQuests, sessionMetrics. No clock, no services. Loadable by desktop Lua 5.1. |
| MachineFlow | module | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/MachineFlow.luau` | The verdict. Calls recordTrial per board row, then recordSession + Badges.checkSession + flush per participant right after scoresCommitted, then fires the shiftreport packet. |
| Badges | module | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/Badges.luau` | Six retention badges off the recordSession receipt. Every id is 0 => fully dark today. Per-userId server-lifetime awarded set. |
| EmoteService | module | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/EmoteService.luau` | Second flush caller (grant / grantPass). Gamepass ownership cache, EmoteEquip/EmotePlay/ShopPrompt remotes, MessagingService subscriber for cross-place grants. |
| MachineState | remote | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/MachineFlow.luau:1420` | The single RemoteEvent carrying every server->client packet kind, including the persistence-relevant `profilestate`, `shiftreport` and `emotestate`. |
| SocialClient | script | `studio-src/StarterPlayer/StarterPlayerScripts/SocialClient.client.luau:78` | [RUNTIME] the ONLY consumer of the shiftreport packet anywhere in the live place; reads packet.openSeats only, ignores xp/clearance/volatile. |
| Main.server (MainGame) | script | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Main.server.luau:18` | Boot order. Profiles.start() runs before EmoteService.start() and MachineFlow.start(), so the profile tables exist before any verdict or emote read. |
| RoomService | module | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/RoomService.luau:307` | Fires RunRequested exactly once per match (phase+token gate), which is what keeps the verdict — and therefore recordSession — from running twice per session. |
| SoloExit | module | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/SoloExit.luau:104` | Teleports the last remaining player to the DEV/menu place. This is the event that empties a MainGame server and therefore races the final save. |
| Telemetry | module | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/Telemetry.luau:17` | AnalyticsService funnel only — NOT persistence. Profiles calls Telemetry.funnel(plr, "KenopsiaRetention", 1, "Joined") after a successful load. |
| PartyRegistry (DEV) | module | `dev-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/PartyRegistry.luau:17` | The DEV place's only store use: a MemoryStore HashMap, not a DataStore. Party-code claim/lookup/release/touch. |
| KenopsiaRooms_v1 | datastore | `dev-src/ReplicatedStorage/Kenopsia/Shared/Config/GameConfig.luau:36` | MemoryStore HashMap (NOT a DataStore). Key = a 6-char code from alphabet "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789"; TTL 3600 s. Holds {accessCode, privateServerId, placeId, createdAt}. |
| PlaylistService (DEV) | module | `dev-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/PlaylistService.luau:166` | ReserveServer + TeleportAsync into MainGame 110672791536316. The entry edge into the only place that persists anything. |
| Main.server (DEV) | script | `dev-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Main.server.luau:5` | Requires ONLY RoomService, PlaylistService, MenuPresenceService — no Profiles, no DataStore. The menu place persists nothing. |
| KenopsiaGrant_v1 | packet | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/EmoteService.luau:89` | MessagingService topic for cross-place emote grants. MainGame subscribes (EmoteService:479); NOTHING in dev-src publishes to it. |
| tests/progression.lua | script | `tests/progression.lua:26` | The only persistence-adjacent test. Loads ProgressionRules ONLY — zero coverage of Profiles' lock, merge, migrate, save or flush timing. |

### Unfinished / dormant

- **No per-player write queue, mutex or in-flight flag anywhere in Profiles. Two UpdateAsync calls on the same key `u_<id>` can be in flight simultaneously, because the only re-entrancy gate — `entry.dirty` — is cleared AFTER the write returns, not when the write starts.** — intentional: no — the file's own header claims writes happen 'at EXACTLY three moments' (Profiles.luau:15-17), which is already false: EmoteService added two more flush callers in Phase 5, and the header was not updated. Nothing in the code serialises them. — Profiles.luau:470 `if not entry or entry.volatile or not entry.dirty or not store then return false end` is flush's whole gate; entry.dirty is only cleared at :145 `if ok then entry.dirty = false end`, i.e. after `store:UpdateAsync` has returned. So a second Profiles.flush called while the first UpdateAsync is still yielding passes the gate and spawns a second trySave (:471-477). Concrete trigger pair: MachineFlow.luau:1325 `pcall(Profiles.flush, e.userId)` in the verdict loop and EmoteService.luau:278 `pcall(Profiles.flush, userId)` fired by MarketplaceService.PromptGamePassPurchaseFinished (EmoteService.luau:459-468) or by the MessagingService subscriber (EmoteService.luau:483).
- **trySave never verifies the session lock it is about to write over. It writes all OWNED_FIELDS unconditionally and then either clears `_lock` or re-stamps it with this server's jobId, regardless of who currently holds it.** — intentional: no — the header advertises 'merge discipline: write OUR fields (OWNED_FIELDS), preserve anything a future schema added' (:18-19), which is about forward-compat schema fields only; lock verification is simply absent and unmentioned. — Profiles.luau:132-143 — the transform function reads `stored`, immediately does `if type(stored) ~= "table" then stored = {} end`, copies OWNED_FIELDS, and sets `_lock` — there is no read of `stored._lock` and no `lockHeldByOther(stored, ...)` check on the save path (lockHeldByOther at :99-104 is called only from tryLoad:112).
- **The session lock has NO per-load unique token. It is {jobId, ts} only, so a write cannot be attributed to a particular load of a particular server — a stale write from an earlier load on the SAME server is indistinguishable from the current one.** — intentional: yes for the cross-server case (the header at :9-10 says exactly '{jobId, ts} session lock (a lock older than LOCK_STALE_SECONDS is stealable)'), no for the same-server case — the same-jobId shortcut at :102 means a rejoin on the same server bypasses the lock entirely, which is what makes the load-window rejoin race below possible. — Profiles.luau:118 `data._lock = { jobId = game.JobId, ts = now }` and :140 `stored._lock = { jobId = game.JobId, ts = os.time() }`. The check is Profiles.luau:102-103 `if lock.jobId == game.JobId then return false end` / `return (now - (tonumber(lock.ts) or 0)) <= LOCK_STALE_SECONDS` — jobId equality plus a 180 s staleness window (:41 `local LOCK_STALE_SECONDS = 180`). No HttpService:GenerateGUID, no counter, no session id anywhere in the file.
- **A slow load DESTROYS progress earned during the load window: the loaded table replaces the live table wholesale, with no merge.** — intentional: no — the header (:4-8) claims 'every player gets an in-memory profile the moment they are seen ... and PLAY only ever touches that table', which reads as if the volatile table is authoritative until upgraded. The wholesale replacement contradicts that and is not mentioned anywhere in the file. — Profiles.luau:244 `entry.data = data` (and the same at :235 on the left-while-loading branch). Everything written into the volatile seed before that line — Profiles.recordTrial writing `entry.data.trialStats[trialId]` (:445-461), Profiles.recordSession writing xp/sessions/streak/quests/crates (:320-402), grantEmote writing `owned[emoteId] = true` (:549) — is dropped on the floor. The window is real: LOAD_ATTEMPTS = 3 (:42) with LOAD_BACKOFF = { 2, 5 } (:43), so up to ~7 s of task.wait plus three UpdateAsync round trips.
- **An inconsistency inside that same overwrite: pendingTrialWins survives a load that wipes the trialStats rows it belongs to, because it hangs off `entry`, not `entry.data`.** — intentional: unclear — the header at :435-441 justifies the session-scoped stash surviving a dead session, but says nothing about a load landing between recordTrial and recordSession. — Profiles.luau:453-458 `local pending = entry.pendingTrialWins ... entry.pendingTrialWins = pending; pending[trialId] = true` — stored on the entry. Line 244 `entry.data = data` replaces only entry.data. So the next verdict's quest metrics (consumed at :357 `local wins = entry.pendingTrialWins or {}`) still credit a trialwin_* that trialStats no longer records.
- **BindToClose does not wait for a PlayerRemoving save that is already in flight, because onLeave removes the entry from `live` BEFORE it starts writing.** — intentional: no — the header (:638-640) says 'Flush every loaded profile in parallel with a bounded wait', which is what it does, but the in-flight leave write is invisible to that bound. — Profiles.luau:275-279 `local entry = live[plr.UserId]` / `live[plr.UserId] = nil` / ... / `local ok, err = trySave(entry, true)` — the entry is out of `live` while the UpdateAsync yields. BindToClose then iterates only `live`: :644-645 `for _, entry in pairs(live) do if not entry.volatile then` and its wait loop is `while waiting > 0` (:655), so with an emptied `live` it computes waiting == 0 and returns immediately. On the SoloExit path (SoloExit.luau:104) the last player teleporting out is exactly when this happens.
- **A load-window rejoin on the SAME server can make the old loader thread release a lock the new load just took, and write the old table over it.** — intentional: no — the comment at :233-234 states the intent as 'Left while we were loading: hand the lock straight back (with lastSeen) and forget the entry', which does not consider the rejoin case. — Profiles.luau:232 `if live[userId] ~= entry or plr.Parent ~= Players then` — this condition is also satisfied when the player left and rejoined, because onPlayer (:215-225) installs a NEW entry under the same userId. The old thread then runs :235-238 `entry.data = data; entry.volatile = false; entry.data.lastSeen = os.time(); trySave(entry, true)` — release=true clears `_lock` while the player is still on this server holding a fresh lock from the second load.
- **There is no idempotency key on the verdict write itself. recordSession blindly increments; nothing records 'this sessionId has already been recorded for this player'.** — intentional: unclear — the quest half WAS deliberately made idempotent (ProgressionRules.luau:260-263 'Claiming on the SAME apply that reached the goal is what makes the payout idempotent: a second apply the same day adds into the clamp and completes nothing'), and tickStreak too (:131-133 `if last ~= 0 and dayStamp <= last then return streak end`). The XP/sessions/viableCount half was not, and no comment explains the asymmetry. — Profiles.luau:323 `data.sessions = (data.sessions or 0) + 1`, :326/:328 `data.viableCount = (data.viableCount or 0) + 1` / `data.rejectedCount = ...`, :399 `data.xp = (data.xp or 0) + xpDelta`. The function takes `summary` (:316) which carries viable/band/roundsPlayed/trialsWon — no session id, and nothing in the profile records one. Today this is saved only by the caller: RoomService.luau:277 `if room.phase ~= "Waiting" then return end` plus the token check at :284/:289 makes runRequested fire once per match, and the verdict is straight-line code in one xpcall (MachineFlow.luau:850 to :1380).
- **The 'cannot save' state is computed, marshalled and sent to the client — and then rendered nowhere. There is no RECORDS OFFLINE surface, or any equivalent, anywhere in the live place.** — intentional: yes as a phase decision — Profiles.luau:171-172 says 'The UI contract, dark until a later phase renders it' — but the consequence is that a player whose profile never loaded plays a whole session, earns nothing durable, and is never told. Server-side the operator gets ONE warn per key per server lifetime (warnOnce at :58-62; keys "store"/"load"/"save"), after which every further failure is silent. — [SOURCE] Profiles.luau:184 `volatile = entry.volatile,` inside the profilestate packet; :428 `volatile = entry.volatile and true or false,` in the recordSession receipt; MachineFlow.luau:1365 `volatile = receipt.volatile,` in the shiftreport packet. [RUNTIME] live MainGame place 110672791536316 via weppy manage_scripts search: pattern "profilestate" over path=game.StarterPlayer -> 0 matches, 28 scripts searched; pattern "shiftreport" -> 1 match, SocialClient line 78, whose handler reads only openSeats (SocialClient.client.luau:79-84); pattern "OFFLINE" over path=game -> 0 matches, 115 scripts searched. The only feedback a volatile player gets is an absence: Profiles.luau:197 `if entry.volatile then return end` silently suppresses the greeting.
- **Zero automated coverage of the persistence layer. The only test that touches progression loads the pure rules module and nothing else.** — intentional: yes by construction — ProgressionRules is deliberately pure so a desktop Lua can prove it (ProgressionRules.luau:4-10), and Profiles is deliberately 'only the traffic and the timing' (:25). But the effect is that the riskiest code in the persistence area is the untested code. — tests/progression.lua:6 `local ROOT = "studio-src/ReplicatedStorage/Kenopsia/Shared/Rules/"` and :26 `local Progression = loadModule("ProgressionRules.luau")`. grep of the whole tests/ directory for "Profiles" returns exactly one hit, a comment at tests/progression.lua:263 ('Profiles.recordSession runs exactly this arithmetic over crates'). Nothing exercises lockHeldByOther, migrate, fillMissing, trySave's merge, OWNED_FIELDS completeness, or flush timing.
- **The documented cross-place grant publisher does not exist. MainGame subscribes to KenopsiaGrant_v1; nothing in dev-src publishes to it, and dev-src writes no DataStore at all.** — intentional: yes as staging (the socket is written for a ceremony not yet built), but it leaves a live subscriber with no publisher, and it means EmoteService.grant's 'a grant for an offline player is LOST' warning (Profiles.luau:536-539, EmoteService.luau:254) is currently unenforced because no durable writer exists on the DEV side to be the source of truth. — EmoteService.luau:471-474 'DEV and MainGame share universe 10640788131 and the KenopsiaProfile_v1 DataStore, so a crate ceremony in DEV publishes here'; subscriber at :479-484. But dev-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Main.server.luau:5-7 requires only RoomService, PlaylistService, MenuPresenceService, and a grep of dev-src for DataStoreService/MessagingService/GetDataStore/SetAsync/UpdateAsync/GetAsync returns only PartyRegistry's MemoryStore lines (3, 17, 56, 90, 114).

### Risks

- LOCK RE-STAMP AFTER RELEASE -> the player is locked out of their own profile for up to 180 s. Profiles.luau:140 `stored._lock = { jobId = game.JobId, ts = os.time() }` runs on every non-release save. If a flush write (release=false, Profiles.luau:473) lands AFTER onLeave's release write (release=true, :279), the stored profile is left locked by a server the player has already left, with a brand-new timestamp. tryLoad on any other server then hits :112 `if lockHeldByOther(stored, now) then blocked = true` and returns 'locked by another server' (:124) for all 3 attempts (LOAD_ATTEMPTS = 3, :42) — the player falls through to permanently VOLATILE for that server, and because flush (:470) and onLeave (:277) both early-return on entry.volatile, NOTHING that session is ever written. NEEDS_RUNTIME to prove the ordering actually inverts; see uncertainties.
- STALE WRITE ONTO A NEWER SESSION -> silent rollback. Because trySave (Profiles.luau:132-143) never checks who holds `_lock`, a late-landing write from server A overwrites every OWNED_FIELD (:92-96: xp, clearance, sessions, streak, quests, emotes, crates, trialStats, ...) on a key that server B has already loaded. If B loaded BEFORE A's write landed, B's in-memory copy is now ahead of a store that just went backwards, and B's own next save will write B's copy — which was built from the pre-A snapshot. Net effect: session A's progress disappears. There is no version counter, no CAS, and no `_lock` comparison to detect it.
- CONCURRENT SAME-KEY WRITES ARE REACHABLE FROM A CLIENT-ADJACENT PATH. EmoteService.luau:459-468 (PromptGamePassPurchaseFinished) and :479-483 (MessagingService subscriber) both end in `pcall(Profiles.flush, userId)` (:278 / :260) with no coordination against MachineFlow.luau:1325's verdict flush. Because the dirty flag is only cleared post-write (Profiles.luau:145), both flushes pass the gate and both spawn a trySave. The payload is the same table reference so the DATA is identical; the divergence is in `_lock` and in DataStore write-throttle pressure on one key.
- LOAD-WINDOW PROGRESS IS DOUBLE-LOST. During the up-to-~7 s load window (LOAD_ATTEMPTS 3 x LOAD_BACKOFF {2,5}, Profiles.luau:42-43) the entry is volatile, so (a) no write can happen at all — flush returns false at :470 and onLeave returns at :277 — and (b) when the stored profile finally lands it is `entry.data = data` (:244), discarding whatever was recorded meanwhile. A player who joins mid-session and reaches a verdict inside that window earns nothing and is told nothing.
- BINDTOCLOSE CAN CUT OFF THE LAST PLAYER'S FINAL SAVE. onLeave removes the entry from `live` before saving (Profiles.luau:276 then :279), and BindToClose only waits on `live` (:644-645, :655). SoloExit teleports the last remaining player out (SoloExit.luau:104), which is precisely when the server begins shutting down — so the very save that matters most (release + lastSeen for the last player) is the one nobody waits for. CLOSE_FLUSH_SECONDS = 25 (:44) protects only entries still in `live`.
- NO SESSION-LEVEL IDEMPOTENCY ON THE XP/COUNTER HALF OF THE VERDICT. recordSession increments sessions, viableCount/rejectedCount and xp unconditionally (Profiles.luau:323, 326/328, 399). The quest payout and the streak tick ARE idempotent (ProgressionRules.luau:301-307; :131-133), so a duplicate verdict would pay no quest XP but WOULD double the session XP and inflate the lifetime counters. Nothing in the profile records which sessionId has been consumed. Today the only thing preventing a double verdict is RoomService's phase/token gate (RoomService.luau:277, 284, 289) — a single-point protection outside the persistence layer.
- SILENT FAILURE BY DESIGN, WITH NO OPERATOR SIGNAL AFTER THE FIRST EVENT. warnOnce (Profiles.luau:58-62) latches per key for the server's lifetime, so a server where every load fails prints ONE line ('load failed (expected in Studio without API access)', :249) and then nothing. There is no counter, no Telemetry event on load/save failure (Telemetry is only called on SUCCESS, :261), and no AnalyticsService signal. A production server silently running fully volatile would look identical to a healthy one from outside.
- MIGRATION IS ONE-WAY AND UNGUARDED AGAINST TYPE CORRUPTION AT THE TOP LEVEL. migrate/fillMissing (Profiles.luau:72-88) only fills MISSING keys; a stored value of the wrong TYPE (e.g. a string where a number belongs) passes straight through. Individual consumers hand-guard the ones that would throw — crates at :380-390, streak via tickStreak, emotes via emoteState (:504-514), quests via applyQuests — but xp, sessions, roundsPlayed, viableCount, rejectedCount, bestBand and clearance are only tonumber'd at point of use, and `data.analytics.d1Stamp` at :341 is dereferenced with no type guard at all (a non-table `analytics` would throw inside recordSession, which the header at :300 promises never happens).

### Uncertainties

- Whether Roblox actually delivers two concurrent same-key UpdateAsync requests from one server in issue order. Every 'late-landing write' risk above depends on it. NEEDS_RUNTIME. The observation that settles it: a play test on the live MainGame server where one player triggers an emote grant (EmoteService.luau:260 flush) and disconnects within the same second, followed by reading key `u_<UserId>` from KenopsiaProfile_v1 and inspecting `_lock`. If `_lock` is non-nil after the player is gone, the flush write landed after the release write and the lockout scenario is real. I could not run this — it requires DataStore access and a running server, both of which are outside a read-only pass.
- Whether the lockout actually reproduces end to end. NEEDS_RUNTIME: two MainGame servers, player leaves A immediately after a verdict and joins B within 180 s, then read B's server log for the warnOnce string 'load failed (expected in Studio without API access): locked by another server' (Profiles.luau:249 formatting :124's message) and check whether Profiles.isVolatile returns true for them on B.
- Whether the load-window overwrite is reachable in real play. It needs a recordTrial/recordSession/grantEmote to land inside the up-to-~7 s window at Profiles.luau:229-254. NEEDS_RUNTIME: instrument nothing, just observe — join a MainGame server with API access throttled, and check whether a player who joins mid-trial has a trialStats row for that trial after their next verdict.
- The freshness of the client-side conclusion. My 'nothing renders volatile' claim is [RUNTIME] against the connected Studio instance (weppy clientId ad22f53b, placeId 110672791536316, studioState edit) — which is the live-place edit session, not a published production server. If the published place is ahead of this Studio session, a shiftreport/profilestate renderer could exist there. The known-hazard list in my brief names KenopsiaClient explicitly as a script where the repo lags live; my search covered the live Studio's 28 StarterPlayer scripts, so it is one step better than the repo, but not a production observation.
- Whether the DEV place currently has any DataStore code that dev-src does not mirror. The weppy connection listed exactly one plugin client, placeId 110672791536316 — the DEV place 129909297895850 was NOT connected, so every DEV claim here is [SOURCE] from dev-src/ only. NEEDS_RUNTIME: connect the DEV Studio and run the same search (pattern GetDataStore over path=game) to confirm 0 matches there.
- Whether the repo mirror of MachineFlow, EmoteService, RoomService and Badges matches live. I verified Profiles specifically ([RUNTIME] live line count 663 = repo 663; live lines 99-148 and 465-479 byte-identical to the repo, and live line 39 is the STORE_NAME line) and the EmoteService/SocialClient line numbers I quote were corroborated by live search hits (EmoteService lines 258/265, SocialClient line 78). I did NOT read live MachineFlow or RoomService — the verdict-loop and match-gate line numbers I quote (MachineFlow 1285/1299-1327/1351-1367, RoomService 277-307) are [SOURCE] only.
- The stale-mirror trap I avoided, recorded so nobody re-walks it: weppy-project-sync/place_110672791536316_Kenopsia_MainGame/.sync-meta.json reports lastFullSync 2026-08-21T14:09:25Z with scriptCount 23, which predates the Phase 4a/4b/4c persistence work (the code carries dated comments from 22.08, 24.08 and 30.08.2026). That mirror contains no Profiles at all and must not be used as evidence about persistence.

## MainGame (placeId 110672791536316) — arrival and lobby: Main.server.luau boot, Lobby, RoomService (canonical room lifecycle / READY consent / abort), SoloExit, CharacterService, GameConfig, Pacing.

MainGame boots one Script (ServerScriptService.KenopsiaServer.Main) that requires 8 modules and then calls 8 .start() functions in a fixed order: Lobby, SoloExit, RoomService, Telemetry, Profiles, EmoteService, CharacterService, MachineFlow [SOURCE Main.server.luau:5-21; RUNTIME live source identical, 24 lines]. Lobby.ensure() builds workspace.KenopsiaLobby and, critically, workspace.SpawnLocation at runtime — neither exists in the saved place [RUNTIME find_child SpawnLocation under game.Workspace = found:false]. RoomService owns exactly one room (id 1) that is never destroyed, only reset; its phase takes five values (Waiting, Starting, Playing, Aborting, Cleanup) and its sub-stage six (nil, Selecting, Briefing, Trial, Score, FinalScore). RoomService creates the 8 Room* remotes at start; ReplicatedStorage.Kenopsia.Remotes likewise does not exist in the saved place [RUNTIME: "Instance not found: game.ReplicatedStorage.Kenopsia.Remotes"]. Seating is NOT driven by Players.PlayerAdded — RoomService connects PlayerAdded nowhere [RUNTIME script search "PlayerAdded" in live RoomService: 0 matches]; the only seating path in shipped code is MachineFlow's 2-second watchdog, which stamps KenopsiaJoinedAt, waits >7 s, then calls RoomService.autoSeat. READY is collected server-authoritatively on RoomReadyRequest and the full who-is-ready set is broadcast to every player on the server inside the RoomState packet's `members` array. A match starts only through maybeAutoStart (RequireAllReady + minPlayers + allReady) → beginCountdown → 3-second countdown → phase Playing → RunRequested:Fire(room.id); the manual RoomStartRequest path exists but no shipped client fires it. Abort is a three-part mechanism: abortCountdown (Starting→Waiting, 3 reason strings), abortMatch (Playing→Aborting, 3 reason strings, double-guarded), and cleanupToWaiting (any non-Waiting phase → Cleanup → Waiting, 4 reason strings from MachineFlow). SoloExit is the only TeleportService call in the whole MainGame tree: it sends the last remaining player to place 129909297895850 the moment headcount drops to 1, provided the server has ever held 2+ players.

### Unfinished / dormant

- **default.project.json's Services map does not contain Lobby, SoloExit, Telemetry, Profiles, EmoteService, Badges or FieldPush — yet Main.server.luau requires five of those by name. A `rojo build default.project.json` produces a place whose boot Script errors on line 8 and boots NOTHING.** — intentional: no — this is repo/place drift. The three newest modules are dated 31.08.2026 in their own headers (Lobby.luau:2, SoloExit.luau:2) i.e. the day before this review, and were evidently added to the place without updating the Rojo manifest. — default.project.json:72-100 enumerates 26 Services entries, none of them named Lobby/SoloExit/Telemetry/Profiles/EmoteService/Badges/FieldPush; Main.server.luau:8-12 `local Telemetry = require(Services.Telemetry) local Profiles = require(Services.Profiles) local EmoteService = require(Services.EmoteService) local Lobby = require(Services.Lobby) local SoloExit = require(Services.SoloExit)`. [RUNTIME] the live place DOES have all 7 (query_instances children of game.ServerScriptService.KenopsiaServer.Services returns 33 modules including Lobby, SoloExit, Telemetry, Profiles, Badges, EmoteService and FieldPush).
- **FieldPush exists as a ModuleScript in the live place but has NO source file anywhere in the repo. It is invisible to review, to git and to any sync.** — intentional: no — a place-only module with no repo copy is exactly the failure mode Lobby.luau:16-21 argues against for geometry. — [RUNTIME] game.ServerScriptService.KenopsiaServer.Services.FieldPush (ModuleScript, sessionDebugId 1_24028). Repo-wide Grep for "FieldPush" across C:\Users\Asus\Claude\Kenopsia_Roblox Project: "No files found".
- **default.project.json maps two client scripts into MainGame that the live place does not have (MainMenu, SimulationGrade), and omits six that it does have (AbortScreen, GradeDirector, LightGuard, RunnerClient, SocialClient, SonarClient). The manifest and the place disagree in both directions.** — intentional: unclear — MainMenu belongs to the Kenopsia_DEV place; its presence in the MainGame manifest looks like a copy/paste leftover, but nothing in the repo says so. — default.project.json:111 `"MainMenu": { "$path": "studio-src/StarterPlayer/StarterPlayerScripts/MainMenu.client.luau" }`, :113 `"SimulationGrade": ...`. [RUNTIME] children of game.StarterPlayer.StarterPlayerScripts = KenopsiaClient, MachineLayout, GoreClient, TrialClientKit, UiFx, Glyphs, MobileProbe, IceTheme, LightGuard, GradeDirector, SonarClient, RunnerClient, SocialClient, AbortScreen, TrialClients — no MainMenu, no SimulationGrade; MobileProbe and IceTheme exist only live.
- **RoomService.finishRun is dead code in MainGame. Its reason string "match complete" can therefore never be printed by this place.** — intentional: unclear — it is a plausible leftover from when the dev and main RoomService were one file (RoomService.luau:2-6 describes the retirement of the multi-room model). — RoomService.luau:548-550 `function RoomService.finishRun(_room) cleanupToWaiting("match complete") end`. Repo Grep for `finishRun` across studio-src returns only those two lines — every other hit is in dev-src/PlaylistService, which talks to the DEV place's own separate RoomService.
- **RoomService.carryReady is dead code. Nothing calls it; the auto-rematch it implemented was deliberately removed on 30.08.2026 but the function was left behind.** — intentional: yes for the behaviour change (both comments state it explicitly), no for leaving the function: it still mutates m.ready and calls maybeAutoStart, so any future caller silently reinstates the removed one-player-starts-for-everyone bug. — RoomService.luau:379 `function RoomService.carryReady(userIds)`; the only other mentions in the whole tree are two comments recording its removal — MachineFlow.luau:491 `-- task.wait(RematchGrace) + RoomServiceRef.carryReady, das die Subjekte` and :1276 `-- carryReady ist dort raus -- JEDER drueckt READY selbst`.
- **GameConfig.Match.RematchGrace (6) is now unreferenced, the counterpart of the removed carryReady call.** — intentional: yes as a behaviour change, no as config hygiene. — GameConfig.luau:90-92 `-- P1.7: seconds after the post-verdict lobby reveal before the last session's subjects are re-armed as ready (time to walk away). RematchGrace = 6,`; MachineFlow.luau:491 records that the `task.wait(RematchGrace)` was deleted.
- **Config.Players.MaximumPerServer (4) and Config.Players.MinimumForMatch (2) are read by nothing. RoomService uses Config.Room.MaxPlayers and Config.Room.MinPlayers instead. Two independent sources of truth for the same two numbers, agreeing only by coincidence.** — intentional: no — GameConfig.luau:11-13 presents MaximumPerServer as the number the published capacity must match, which implies it is load-bearing. It is not. — Repo Grep over studio-src for `MaximumPerServer|MinimumForMatch|StudioTestMinimum|Config\.Players` returns exactly 4 lines: GameConfig.luau:14, :15, :17 (the definitions) and RoomService.luau:95 `return Config.Players.StudioTestMinimum` — StudioTestMinimum is the ONLY one of the three ever read.
- **The four legacy seating remotes are created, rate-limited and connected on the server, but no shipped client fires any of them. Same for RoomStartRequest: the manual-start path with its three distinct error strings is unreachable from the shipped UI.** — intentional: yes for the four join shims (RoomService.luau:5-6 `the create/join/quick remotes survive only as shims`), NO/unstated for RoomStartRequest — nothing documents that manual start was retired. — RoomService.luau:594-631 connects all five. Repo Grep for `RoomCreateRequest|RoomJoinRequest|RoomQuickJoinRequest|RoomLeaveRequest|RoomStartRequest` across studio-src returns hits ONLY inside RoomService.luau — the client files match only on RoomState (KenopsiaClient:51), RoomReadyRequest (:53, :3186) and LobbyError.
- **abortCountdown returns the room to Waiting without going through Cleanup, so it clears neither room.stage nor the ready flags. A countdown aborted while stage=="Selecting" leaves the room Waiting with stage still "Selecting".** — intentional: unclear — it may be deliberate (the lobby really is still showing the selection), but no comment says so, while the adjacent toWaiting is heavily commented about exactly this class of residue (:209-211 `Progress belongs to the match that just ended`). — RoomService.luau:262-274 — the whole function writes only token, phase and countdown; compare toWaiting at :204 `room.stage = nil` and :222 `clearReady()`, neither of which abortCountdown calls.
- **Nothing rescues a room stranded in phase "Aborting" or "Cleanup". abortMatch sets Aborting and returns; only MachineFlow's runMatch finalizer calls cleanupToWaiting, and MachineFlow's own 2 s watchdog acts on Playing and Waiting only.** — intentional: partly — MachineFlow.luau:1403-1404 claims `Reached on every route, including no-trial and error, so the room can never be stranded in Aborting`, which is true only while runMatch is alive. If RunRequested fires and runMatch is never spawned (getRoom returns nil, or MachineFlow.start never ran because an earlier require threw), Playing/Aborting is terminal with no recovery. — RoomService.luau:239-251 abortMatch never calls toWaiting/cleanupToWaiting; MachineFlow.luau:1405 is the sole cleanupToWaiting caller and lives inside runMatch; MachineFlow.luau:1461 `if room and room.phase == "Playing"` and :1497 `for _, room in RoomServiceRef.waitingRooms() do` with RoomService.luau:434-437 `if room.phase == "Waiting" then return { room } end return {}`.

### Risks

- [RUNTIME] SERVER CAPACITY IS 60, NOT 4. The live MainGame Edit datamodel reports Players.MaxPlayers = 60 (weppy manage_properties get on game.Players, placeId 110672791536316), while GameConfig.luau:11-14 states `Published capacity must match this ... MaximumPerServer = 4` and the room seats 4. With a 60-cap server, players 5..60 are permanently spectators: seat() sends them to addSpectator (RoomService.luau:401-405) and promoteSpectators only takes them while #members < 4 (:189). It also disarms SoloExit entirely, because headcount never reaches 1. NEEDS_RUNTIME to settle whether the PUBLISHED server size differs from the Edit-datamodel value: read Players.MaxPlayers from a running live server (or open File > Game Settings > Places > Server Size). This is the single highest-impact finding in the area.
- [SOURCE] SEATING HAS A SINGLE POINT OF FAILURE AND A KILL SWITCH. RoomService connects Players.PlayerAdded nowhere ([RUNTIME] script search of the live RoomService for 'PlayerAdded': 0 matches), so the ONLY way anyone is ever seated is MachineFlow.luau:1456 `RoomServiceRef.autoSeat(plr)` inside a loop wrapped in `if not workspace:GetAttribute("KenopsiaNoAuto") then` (:1447). Setting that one workspace attribute, or any error that prevents MachineFlow.start from reaching line 1444, means nobody is ever seated, no READY is ever accepted (setReady rejects non-members with 'SPECTATING - NEXT MATCH', :352-355) and no match can ever start. [RUNTIME] the attribute is currently unset (game.Workspace attributeCount 0), so the path is live today.
- [SOURCE] SoloExit's `hadCompany` latches TRUE FOR THE LIFE OF THE SERVER and is never reset (set at SoloExit.luau:117, :135, :137; no assignment to false anywhere in the file). Consequence: once two players have been on a server, any later solo occupant is teleported out. Concretely — a 2-player party finishes, both leave, the server lingers, one new player joins alone: headcount 1 and hadCompany still true, so the 5-second watch (:151-156) bounces them within 5 s. The module's own docstring (:27-35) argues hadCompany distinguishes 'allein UEBRIG' from 'allein ANGEKOMMEN', but a latched flag cannot make that distinction after the original company has gone. NEEDS_RUNTIME: join a live MainGame server that previously held 2+ players, alone, and observe whether the '[SoloExit] armed'/teleport path fires.
- [SOURCE] SoloExit fires BEFORE RoomService learns the player left. Main.server.luau:15 runs SoloExit.start() and :16 runs RoomService.start(), so SoloExit's Players.PlayerRemoving handler (SoloExit.luau:140) is connected first and Roblox invokes connections in registration order. On a 2-player match where one quits, the survivor's TeleportAsync is issued before removeMember reaches abortMatch("PLAYER LEFT - CANNOT CONTINUE WITH A SINGLE PLAYER") (RoomService.luau:340). The AbortScreen card (AbortScreen.client.luau:93) may therefore be raced by the teleport and never read. NEEDS_RUNTIME: a 2-player live test where one player quits mid-trial, watching whether the survivor sees the abort card before the loading screen.
- [SOURCE] Lobby.start() is called at Main.server.luau:14, i.e. AFTER eight requires (:5-12) that each execute module bodies containing WaitForChild calls (e.g. EmoteService.luau:56-58, Profiles.luau:36, RoomService.luau:24). Any one of those yielding lets a joining player spawn before workspace.SpawnLocation exists — which is precisely the failure Lobby.luau:6-14 was written to end (character falls to FallenPartsDestroyHeight -500, respawns into the same hole, `player.Character == nil` for the whole session). The comment on :14 asserts Lobby must run first, but it does not run first; eight module loads do.
- [SOURCE] GameConfig.Room.RequireAllReady is an ON/OFF switch that INVERTS badly. maybeAutoStart's first line is `if not Config.Room.RequireAllReady then return end` (RoomService.luau:312) — setting it false does not relax the ready requirement, it disables auto-start completely, and since RoomStartRequest is never fired by a shipped client, the game would become unstartable. The flag reads as 'require all ready = false means start with fewer', which is the opposite of what it does.
- [SOURCE] Studio and live take DIFFERENT minimums from DIFFERENT config tables. minPlayers() returns Config.Players.StudioTestMinimum (=1) under RunService:IsStudio() and Config.Room.MinPlayers (=2) otherwise (RoomService.luau:93-98). Every Studio play test therefore runs a solo path — requiredMinimum becomes math.min(#members, 1) = 1 (:300), so removeMember's abort threshold at :335 only trips at zero remaining, and SoloExit prints instead of teleporting (:95-102). The 2-player abort path and the solo-exit path are BOTH unexercisable in Studio. NEEDS_RUNTIME: they can only be proven on a published server with two real clients.
- [SOURCE] KenopsiaClient acquires LobbyError with FindFirstChild, not WaitForChild (KenopsiaClient.client.luau:3122), and ensureRemotes creates RoomState before LobbyError (RoomService.luau:567). The client survives only because line 52 waits for MachineState, which MachineFlow.start creates last (Main.server.luau:21) — an accidental barrier. Any reordering of Main's start() calls silently makes every server rejection invisible, which is the exact bug the block's own comment (:3110-3115) says it exists to fix. AbortScreen does it correctly (`WaitForChild("LobbyError", 30)`, AbortScreen.client.luau:90). NOTE: KenopsiaClient is on the known-stale list — this is [SOURCE] from the repo mirror and NEEDS_RUNTIME confirmation against the live LocalScript.
- [SOURCE] Two independent participant snapshots are taken at two different moments and can disagree. RoomService takes room.participants at the end of the countdown (:298-299) and sets requiredMinimum there (:300); MachineFlow takes ctx.participantUserIds and ctx.audience separately when runMatch actually runs (MachineFlow.luau:297-301), after RunRequested has round-tripped through a BindableEvent and a task.spawn. A player who leaves in that window is in one list and not the other.
- [SOURCE] room.spectators is broadcast as a bare array of userIds with no display names (RoomService.luau:106-109, :120), while members carry displayName. A client cannot render a benched player's name from the RoomState packet — KenopsiaClient uses the array only to decide `amSpectator` for itself (:2054-2057). Any 'who else is waiting' UI would need a second lookup.

### Uncertainties

- Everything I state about KenopsiaClient.client.luau is [SOURCE] from the repo mirror, which the task brief flags as BEHIND the live place. NEEDS_RUNTIME: read game.StarterPlayer.StarterPlayerScripts.KenopsiaClient live and diff the RoomState.OnClientEvent handler (repo lines 2040-2075) and updateRoster (1988-2027). Until then, the claim 'the roster renders a per-member ready checkmark' is unproven for the shipped client.
- The published server size is unconfirmed. I read Players.MaxPlayers = 60 from the Edit datamodel [RUNTIME], which normally mirrors the place's saved server size, but the authoritative value lives in Game Settings on the published place and I cannot read it here. NEEDS_RUNTIME: print Players.MaxPlayers from a running live server of place 110672791536316, or open File > Game Settings > Places > Server Size.
- I did not diff the live MachineFlow against the repo copy. All MachineFlow line references (the seating watchdog at 1444-1505, preFlow at 451-509, the finalizer at 1382-1413) are [SOURCE]. The live RoomService, SoloExit, Main and GameConfig ARE confirmed at matching line numbers [RUNTIME], so MachineFlow is likely in parity too — but likely is not evidence. NEEDS_RUNTIME: script search the live MachineFlow for 'autoSeat' and 'cleanupToWaiting' and check the reported line numbers are 1456 and 1405.
- Whether the 'Aborting dead-end' is actually reachable. I proved [SOURCE] that no code path returns a room from Aborting/Cleanup to Waiting except MachineFlow's runMatch finalizer, but I could not construct a reachable case where RunRequested fires and runMatch does not. NEEDS_RUNTIME: force it — abort a match while the runMatch thread is confirmed dead (e.g. via the console) and read back room.phase.
- Whether default.project.json is actually used to build or sync this place, or whether the team syncs selected files only. The manifest's omissions are only fatal if someone runs `rojo build` / a full sync from it. NEEDS_RUNTIME/ASK: check the team's sync procedure and whether weppy-project-sync/ (a directory in the repo root I did not open) supersedes the Rojo manifest.
- The real-world reachability of the SoloExit ping-pong. It depends on whether MainGame servers are ever joined outside a reserved-server teleport from the menu. PlaylistService always reserves (dev-src/.../PlaylistService.luau:166-169), which would prevent a stranger joining a lingering server — but MainGame is a public place in the universe and I could not verify whether direct/social joins are disabled. NEEDS_RUNTIME/ASK: check the place's access settings.
- Roblox does not formally guarantee that RemoteEvent/signal connections fire in registration order. My claim that SoloExit's PlayerRemoving handler runs before RoomService's rests on the documented-in-practice behaviour plus the start() order in Main.server.luau:15-16. NEEDS_RUNTIME: instrument both handlers with timestamped prints on a live 2-player server.
- I did not verify what CharacterService does when GameConfig.Character.UsePS1Rig is true but StarterPlayer.StarterCharacter is missing beyond the warn at CharacterService.luau:68 — specifically whether the lobby is then populated by default Roblox avatars whose scale/hip-height branch (:47-54, gated on the KenopsiaPS1 attribute) is skipped. NEEDS_RUNTIME: inspect game.StarterPlayer for a StarterCharacter child and read its KenopsiaPS1 attribute.

## Session spine — studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/MachineFlow.luau (1514 lines), read in full, plus RoomService.luau, Contexts.luau, Pacing.luau, AudienceCam.luau, Podium.luau, TrialKit.luau and the (STALE) KenopsiaClient.client.luau mirror.

MachineFlow owns everything inside RoomService's Playing phase and drives one linear beat chain: lobby preFlow (roulette -> info card) -> runMatch -> per trial (Selecting -> Briefing -> Trial) -> per round (round card -> optional role card -> hide -> cover -> respawn grace -> guarded round -> cover -> standings -> settle) -> trial score board -> FinalScore verdict -> ShiftReport -> a single finalizer. There are 11 distinct hold() call sites, 17 early-return points inside the xpcall body plus 2 in preFlow, and 10 distinct packet kinds leaving this file (selection, info, status, round, rolecard, hide, cover, score, shiftreport, audiencecam). runRoundGuarded returns (ok, result) on a completed round, (false, "timeout") when its RoundSeconds+35s budget expires, and (false, "aborted") when it observes room.aborting; both call sites treat anything that is not exactly "timeout" as a fatal error and re-raise it, so the ONLY producer of "aborted" (a player leaving mid-round, via RoomService.removeMember -> abortMatch) drives outcome = "error" and reaches the RoomServiceRef.abort(room, "MINIGAME ERROR") call at line 1398 — but that call is a guaranteed no-op, because abortMatch's first guard rejects any phase that is not Playing/Starting and the room is already "Aborting". So a player-cancellation reaches the MINIGAME ERROR call site but never its effect; the players see the earlier "RUN ABORTED - PLAYER LEFT..." card instead, while the server log mislabels the session as "failed: aborted" and cleans up with reason "error". The ShiftReport hold does send a packet (kind="shiftreport", per participant, line 1351) immediately before it, but "shiftreport" is absent from the client's KNOWN_KINDS in the mirror, so it renders nothing and the screen simply holds the previous final-score board for 5 extra seconds — dead air, not a blank screen. The podium beat was deleted (lines 1373-1379) but Podium.luau is still required at line 45 and never called, Pacing.Timing.Podium is orphaned, and — critically — the client's SHIFT COMPLETE end card (the PLAY AGAIN / BACK TO MENU rematch UI) is gated on receiving a kind=="podium" clear packet that nothing sends any more. Rematch is therefore not a vote at all: room.rematch only feeds a retention funnel, RoomService.carryReady is dead code, and the next session starts only when every seated member (including newly promoted bench players) presses READY.

### Nodes

| id | kind | file | role |
|---|---|---|---|
| MachineFlow | module | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/MachineFlow.luau` | The session spine. Owns preFlow (lobby presentation), runMatch (trial loop), runRoundGuarded (round watchdog) and the single finalizer. Started once from Main.server.luau:21. |
| RoomService | service | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/RoomService.luau` | Canonical single room, 2-4 players. Owns phase (Waiting/Starting/Playing/Aborting/Cleanup), sessionId, token, members, spectators, aborting flag, and the cancelled() predicate MachineFlow polls. |
| Contexts | module | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/Contexts.luau` | Session/Trial/Round/Leg token + cleanup-scope tree. MachineFlow creates the whole tree but never hands a scope to a trial module, so every scope it runs is empty (Gates 2/3/4 not done). |
| TrialKit | module | `studio-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Services/TrialKit.luau` | Shared arena/messaging kit. MachineFlow uses only TrialKit.abortRoom (watchdog teardown) and TrialKit.HOME_FALLBACK (the (0,5,0) teleport fallback). |
| Pacing | module | `studio-src/ReplicatedStorage/Kenopsia/Shared/Rules/Pacing.luau` | Pure rules module: ROUNDS/LEGS per player count, RoundSeconds per trial, and the Pacing.Timing table every hold() reads. |
| Playlist | module | `studio-src/ReplicatedStorage/Kenopsia/Shared/Rules/Playlist.luau` | Seeded shuffle (order/session/arrange). Consumed by runSelection (first trial) and playableOrder (the whole session order). |

### Unfinished / dormant

- **A player-cancellation is routed through the ERROR branch. runRoundGuarded returns (false, "aborted") only when room.aborting is true, and the ONLY producer of room.aborting is RoomService.abortMatch — reached from removeMember when a participant leaves and the survivors fall below requiredMinimum. Both call sites treat any non-"timeout" second value as fatal and re-raise it, so a player leaving mid-round makes outcome = "error" and reaches RoomServiceRef.abort(room, "MINIGAME ERROR") at line 1398. PROOF THAT IT CANNOT SHOW: RoomService.abort -> abortMatch's first guard is `if room.phase ~= "Playing" and room.phase ~= "Starting" then return false end`, and room.phase was set to "Aborting" at RoomService.luau:243 in the same resumption (no yield) as the room.aborting = true at :242 that the watchdog observed. Nothing sets phase back to Playing before the finalizer: the only writer is beginCountdown (RoomService.luau:303), which needs phase == "Waiting", i.e. toWaiting, i.e. the cleanupToWaiting call that comes AFTER line 1398. So the MINIGAME ERROR abort is always a no-op on this route — no second LobbyError, no second warn from RoomService. The real damage is a mislabelled log (`[MachineFlow] session S… failed: aborted` + a debug.traceback) and cleanupToWaiting("error") printing `[RoomService] cleanup (error)` for what is a normal player quit.** — intentional: no — the comment at MachineFlow.luau:760-766 explicitly designed the aborting poll so that "der Finalizer schickt alle nach Hause", but it never gave "aborted" its own branch next to "timeout". The three `never "MINIGAME ERROR"` guarantees in the file header (lines 44, 51, 59) show the author's intent that only a real trial crash may take this route. — MachineFlow.luau:782 `return false, room.aborting and "aborted" or "timeout"`; :1111 `if legScores == "timeout" then timedOut = true else error(legScores, 0) end`; :1118 same on the non-bird path; :1384-1385 `if not ok then outcome = "error"`; :1396-1398 `warn(("[MachineFlow] session %s failed: %s") ... RoomServiceRef.abort(room, "MINIGAME ERROR")`; RoomService.luau:240-243 and :340

The 5 s Pacing.Timing.ShiftReport hold that follows is real, so the session is 5 s longer for a beat that draws nothing new.** — intentional: yes — declared as a dark ship in the comment at MachineFlow.luau:1332-1345 and in Pacing.luau:176-182. ### Risks

- HARD LOCK IN THE LOBBY, NO TIMEOUT. After a session, toWaiting() clears every ready flag (RoomService.luau:222 `clearReady()`), and the next session needs Config.Room.RequireAllReady = true plus allReady() over ALL seated members (RoomService.luau:311-316). A single seated player who never presses READY blocks the room forever: there is no rematch timer, RoomLeaveRequest is a deliberate no-op that only re-broadcasts state (RoomService.luau:608-611), and promoteSpectators() has just added every bench player as a full member who must also ready up (RoomService.luau:186-194, called from toWaiting at :221). Rematch is therefore unanimous over the CURRENT seat list, not over the last session's participants.
- runRoundGuarded cannot distinguish a watchdog timeout from a trial that errors with the literal string "timeout". MachineFlow.luau:1111/1118 compare the second return value against "timeout" by equality, and case 1 (line 784 `return ok, result`) puts a raw pcall error message in that same slot. A trial raising error("timeout") would be silently swallowed as a soft round cut instead of aborting the match.
- The Bird Hunting hunter seat is chosen from the LIVE room.members, not from the frozen participant snapshot: `hunter = room.members[(roundIndex - 1) % #room.members + 1]` (MachineFlow.luau:1023). With 4 players, one leaving mid-trial keeps the match alive (requiredMinimum is min(#members, 2) = 2) but re-indexes the rotation, so a seat can be served twice or skipped. Everything else in the loop — scoring, standings, the final board — uses ctx.participantUserIds, which is snapshotted once at MachineFlow.luau:298-301.
- Only CanteenProtocol exposes a cleanup(ctx); BirdHunting and Minefield expose only abort(). module.abort is called ONLY on the watchdog/abort route inside runRoundGuarded (MachineFlow.luau:778-780). If the session is cancelled during a hold() rather than during a round, cleanup() at :809-811 calls activeTrial.module.cleanup, which is nil for those two, and the Contexts scopes it runs are empty — so nothing in this file tears their arena down on that route.
- In Studio, minPlayers() returns Config.Players.StudioTestMinimum = 1 (RoomService.luau:93-98), so requiredMinimum becomes 1 and removeMember never aborts. The whole "aborted" -> outcome="error" path is therefore INVISIBLE in Studio testing and only appears in a live server. Any play test of this finding must be run live, or with StudioTestMinimum temporarily at 2.
- hold() polls at 0.1 s and calls task.wait(0.1) after each check, so every hold overshoots its Pacing value slightly and a cancellation is observed up to ~100 ms late (MachineFlow.luau:355-362). The one wait that is NOT cancellation-aware is preFlow's `task.wait(Pacing.Timing.LobbyReveal or 6.4)` at line 479 — a countdown starting during those 6.4 s is caught only by the phase guard at line 480 afterwards.
- ctx.audience is a snapshot of Player INSTANCES taken once at match start (MachineFlow.luau:297 via RoomService.audience). tell(), setActiveTrial() and setTrialId() all iterate it and filter on `plr.Parent == Players`. A participant who disconnects and rejoins the same server within the session gets a new Player instance and would receive no further Machine packets and no attribute writes for the rest of that session.
- A watchdog-cut round still counts toward the XP math: roundsRun += 1 at MachineFlow.luau:1151 executes before `if timedOut then break end` at :1159, and roundsRun is what Profiles.recordSession receives as roundsPlayed (:1304).

### Uncertainties

- NEEDS_RUNTIME — everything I state about the client is [SOURCE] from studio-src/StarterPlayer/StarterPlayerScripts/KenopsiaClient.client.luau, which the task brief flags as BEHIND the live place. The three client claims that change the conclusions are: (a) 'shiftreport' missing from KNOWN_KINDS (line 37); (b) the cover handler reading p.seconds instead of hold/maxSeconds (line 2902); (c) the SHIFT COMPLETE end card gated on a podium packet (lines 3295-3301). Settle all three with one observation: mcp__Roblox_Studio__script_read on StarterPlayerScripts.KenopsiaClient in the connected MainGame instance, grepping for KNOWN_KINDS, `p.seconds`, and `p.kind == "podium"`.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME — whether the ShiftReport hold is dead air or a real screen. My conclusion is that it is NOT a blank screen: a packet IS sent immediately before it (MachineFlow.luau:1351-1367) but renders nothing, and showScore performs no timed hide (KenopsiaClient.client.luau:2269-2330 has no auto-hide), so the kind='score' verdict board from line 1271 simply stays up for FinalScore 6.0 s + ShiftReport 5.0 s = 11 s. Settle it by playing a 2-player session to the verdict and watching the Output for `[Kenopsia] unhandled MachineState kind: shiftreport` (one per client) plus a stopwatch on how long the verdict board stands.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME — proof of the abort route end to end. Run a live (non-Studio) 2-player session, have one player leave mid-round, and read the server log. The predicted signature is, in order: `[RoomService] aborting match: PLAYER LEFT - CANNOT CONTINUE WITH A SINGLE PLAYER`, then `[MachineFlow] <trial> round cut by match abort`, then `[MachineFlow] session S… failed: aborted` with a traceback, then `[RoomService] cleanup (error)` — and NO second `aborting match: MINIGAME ERROR` line and no second RUN ABORTED card. If a `MINIGAME ERROR` line does appear, my no-op proof is wrong and there is a phase window I did not find.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME — GUI objects. I could not verify from source that machine.Info.Btn_READY, the Warning/SettingsPanel frames, or the EndCard's parent actually exist in the live place's PlayerGui hierarchy, nor that workspace.SpawnLocation exists (cleanup at MachineFlow.luau:836 falls back to TrialKit.HOME_FALLBACK = (0,5,0), which the Lobby.luau:8 comment describes as a hole players fall through and die in). Settle with inspect_instance on workspace.SpawnLocation and a search_game_tree for the Machine ScreenGui children.
- I did not verify the internals of Scoring.rankRound / Scoring.distribute, Playlist.order/session/arrange, MachineVoice.pick, Profiles.recordSession's receipt shape, or Badges.checkSession — I read only their call signatures at the MachineFlow boundary. If the beat list's scoring claims matter (the 1700 pool, doubled on the FINAL AUDIT), Scoring.luau and Pacing.TrialPointPool (Pacing.luau:187) still need reading.
- I did not confirm whether any trial module writes room.aborting or calls RoomService.abort on its own; my repo grep for `abortMatch|RoomService.abort|.aborting` returned only MachineFlow, RoomService and one SoloExit comment, so I believe removeMember is the only producer — but the grep was over studio-src only, and a live-place script that is not mirrored could add a producer. That would widen the set of things that reach the 'aborted' return.
- dev-src/ (the Kenopsia_DEV main-menu place) was not examined at all. If the menu place has its own copy of any of these services, none of my claims cover it.

## Client side of the server→client packet contract — KenopsiaClient, MachineLayout, SocialClient, AbortScreen, ReplicatedFirst/KenopsiaLoading, Shared/Net/Envelope (MainGame, placeId 110672791536316)

The whole client is driven by ONE RemoteEvent, `ReplicatedStorage.Kenopsia.Remotes.MachineState`, carrying a flat `{kind = "..."}` union; four separate LocalScripts each open their own `OnClientEvent` on it (KenopsiaClient's main dispatch, KenopsiaClient's own end-card block, SocialClient, RunnerClient, SonarClient). KenopsiaClient dispatches 28 kinds through a hand-written if-chain, then a whitelist `K.KNOWN_KINDS` (8 screen kinds) gates the screen funnel; anything unrecognised is warned once and dropped. [RUNTIME] I reached the live place through the weppy bridge (clientId ad22f53b-…, placeId 110672791536316, studioState "edit") and read live source directly, so the known mirror staleness is resolved for every claim below — and it mattered: the mirror is missing the live `feed`, `hud` and `fieldPush` handlers entirely. The live set difference is small and exact: SENT BUT NOT HANDLED = `shiftreport`, `profilestate`, `emotestate` (all three server-authored as deliberately "ships dark"); HANDLED BUT NEVER SENT = `mines` and `podium`, plus `trial` which is handled by a full router + 12 client modules that can never fire because all twelve TrialKit trials are `ready = false`. The removed podium beat is load-bearing in the worst way: the SHIFT COMPLETE end card — the only PLAY AGAIN / BACK TO MENU affordance in the game — is gated behind a `podium` CLEAR packet that no server code can produce, so it is dead UI. There is no return-to-menu path on the client at all: [RUNTIME] zero `TeleportService` references across all 26 live StarterPlayerScripts. KenopsiaLoading is bounded except for two unbounded yields (`game.Loaded:Wait()` and `ContentProvider:PreloadAsync`) and pays a guaranteed 10 s `WaitForChild("Warning", 10)` that can never succeed because the `Warning` frame does not exist; KenopsiaClient pays the same 10 s again at top level. SocialClient fires the Roblox invite sheet on `shiftreport` — which lands exactly 6 s into the final standings card, over results that are still on screen. `Shared/Net/Envelope` is dead: [RUNTIME] across 115 live scripts nothing requires it.
