### Unfinished / dormant

- **The SHIFT COMPLETE end card — the game's only PLAY AGAIN / BACK TO MENU surface — is unreachable. It shows only on a `podium` CLEAR packet, and the podium beat was deleted server-side, so no `podium` packet of any kind can be produced.** — intentional: no — the removal was intentional and documented, but the client half was not removed with it. The MachineFlow comment says the intended replacement is "Endstand -> SHIFT REPORT -> Lobby, wo READY der Play-again-Knopf ist", i.e. the author believed READY replaced the card. Nobody deleted the card, so ~130 lines of end-card UI plus the podium camera path (KenopsiaClient:3692, showPodium at mirror:2463) now ship dead. — LIVE KenopsiaClient:4112-4117 "elseif p.kind == \"podium\" and lastFinal then ... if pos == nil then ... task.delay(0.4, show)" is the only call to show(). The sender: MachineFlow.luau:1373-1379 "30.08.2026 (user): DER PODIUMS-BEAT IST RAUS. Hier stand P1.3 -- Podium.raise/strike, 8 s WINNER/2ND-PLACE-Bloecke mit fester Kamera." [RUNTIME] weppy search 'Podium.raise' over game.ServerScriptService (34 scripts) → 2 hits, both the definition (Podium:160) and that comment (MachineFlow:1374). Zero call sites.
- **Even if it were reachable, BACK TO MENU does not go to the menu. It calls ReadyRemote:FireServer(false) — an un-ready — and nothing else. There is no teleport on the client at all.** — intentional: unclear — the button's own comment (mirror KenopsiaClient:3181-3182) describes it as "BACK TO MENU (un-ready; RoomService drops the player from the pending rematch)", so the label was always a lie about a same-place action. Given the label, a player pressing it expects to leave; SoloExit exists precisely because "es im ganzen Spiel keinen einzigen TeleportService-Aufruf gab" (SoloExit.luau:8). — LIVE KenopsiaClient:4041-4045 "local function dismiss(ready) / if card then card.Visible = false end / pcall(function() ReadyRemote:FireServer(ready) end) / sfx(\"Click\") end" — the whole handler. [RUNTIME] weppy search 'TeleportService' over the 26 live StarterPlayerScripts → matchesFound: 0. The only MainGame→menu route is server-side and automatic: SoloExit.luau:104 "TeleportService:TeleportAsync(SoloExit.MENU_PLACE_ID, { plr })", which fires only when exactly one player is left.
- **`shiftreport` ships completely dark and costs a real 5 s of session time on a screen showing nothing new.** — intentional: yes — explicitly ship-dark by design, called out in three places (MachineFlow:1336, Pacing:176-182, Profiles:171). But the 5 s hold is charged today for a beat that renders nothing, and the only thing the player actually sees in that window is the invite popup. — MachineFlow.luau:1336-1339 says so itself: "'shiftreport' is NOT in the client's dispatch yet and an unknown kind renders NOTHING, so this ships dark; the HOLD is real, though: the session grows by Pacing.Timing.ShiftReport (5 s)." Confirmed live: [RUNTIME] weppy search 'shiftreport' over LIVE KenopsiaClient → matchesFound: 0. Pacing.luau:182 "ShiftReport = 5.0,". The 11 fields in the packet (xpDelta, clearance, streak, questsCompleted, fragments, keys...) are all discarded except openSeats, which SocialClient reads.
- **`profilestate` and `emotestate` are the same pattern: full state packets sent every session with no client renderer anywhere.** — intentional: yes — both are documented as deliberately dark. Worth listing because they are the second and third members of the same set as shiftreport: three of 29 sent kinds render nothing. — Profiles.luau:171-178 "The UI contract, dark until a later phase renders it: an unknown kind renders NOTHING on the client" / "kind = \"profilestate\",". EmoteService.luau:302-304 "dark until a later phase renders it ... So \"emotestate\" ships with ZERO client edits." [RUNTIME] weppy search over the 26 live StarterPlayerScripts → 0 matches for either.
- **`mines` handler with no sender, and a fully built trial router + 12 client modules that can never fire.** — intentional: yes for the router (MP-05 staged work, explicitly gated by two independent flags — MachineFlow:76-78). No for `mines`: it is straightforward dead code left behind by the Phase 3b rewrite. — LIVE KenopsiaClient:3430 "if p.kind == \"mines\" then" → showMines(p.list or {}); retired server-side per Minefield.luau:510 "Phase 3b: die Antwort ist nicht mehr das per-player {kind=\"mines\"}". For the router: TrialKit.luau:312 "p.kind = \"trial\"" is the only producer, and [RUNTIME] weppy search 'ready = true' over LIVE MachineFlow → exactly 3 hits (lines 88, 100, 130 = birdhunt, minefield, canteen). All twelve TrialKit trials are ready=false.
- **The client's `hitmark` handler carries a comment asserting it never runs. It does — BirdHunting has sent hitmark since Phase 3c.** — intentional: no — a stale comment that survived the Phase 3c wiring. Harmless at runtime (the handler works and HitMark exists in StarterGui) but it will mislead the next person auditing this dispatch. — LIVE KenopsiaClient:3615-3618 "NOTE: nothing sends kind = \"hitmark\" today, so this handler never runs. Wiring the sniper's hit feedback is its own Gate 3 step". Contradicted by BirdHunting.luau:1206-1208 "Phase 3c: the hitmark handler comes alive. KenopsiaClient has rendered {kind=\"hitmark\"} since Gate 1 ... and nothing ever sent it -- its own comment says so." plus four live sends at BirdHunting:1143, 1218, 1222, 1229.
- **`Shared/Net/Envelope` — the documented anti-replay wire gate — is required by nothing on either side. The clients hand-roll their own packet shapes instead, and none of them carry sessionId or trialToken.** — intentional: unclear — the module header calls itself "ENVELOPE (Gate 1) -- the shape every client packet must have, and the gate it must pass before any trial code looks at it", and it is Rojo-mapped into the place (default.project.json → /ReplicatedStorage/Kenopsia/Shared/Net/Envelope). Either the adoption step was never done or it was reverted; nothing in the source says which. — [RUNTIME] weppy search 'Envelope' over `game` (115 live scripts): hits only inside the module itself plus two comments in Shared.Rules.MachineCam (:9, :80). The nearest thing to it is TrialClientKit.luau:200-207 "deps.TrialInput:FireServer({ trialId = trialId, action = action, roundToken = token, seq = seq, v = 1, data = data, })" — a lookalike missing two of Envelope.validate's four identity fields. The legacy paths send even less: KenopsiaClient:1688-1692 sends {trialId, action, roundToken} with no v and no seq at all. Only tests/envelope.lua loads the module, via its own file loader.
- **The content warning never appears. `KenopsiaMachine.Warning` does not exist and no code creates it, yet two scripts wait 10 s for it on every join.** — intentional: no — MachineLayout.client.luau:32 lists 8 FULLSCREEN frames and 7 exist; Warning is the only member with no counterpart. That is the signature of a frame deleted from the place, not one never authored. For a game whose own client ships a gore budget tuned to "~14+" (GoreClient:25-30), an age-gate screen that cannot render is a shipping-blocker, not a cosmetic gap. — [RUNTIME] (live-gui.md, triple-confirmed) children of StarterGui.KenopsiaMachine = 15, none named Warning; find_child → {"found": false}; search_name('Warning', root=game) → 1 hit, game.SoundService.KenopsiaAudio.SFX.Warning, className Sound. Waited on at LIVE KenopsiaLoading:254 and LIVE KenopsiaClient:3751. Contrast HunterLookSetup, which IS built at runtime (KenopsiaClient mirror:171-186 "lookPanel.Name = \"HunterLookSetup\" ... lookPanel.Parent = machine") — so it is genuinely fine and only Warning is missing.
- **The EndCard, once shown, is not cleared by any screen change — hideAll() does not touch it. It would sit at ZIndex 94 over the lobby until a round starts.** — intentional: no — latent, and currently masked by the fact that the card never shows. Restoring the podium beat would immediately expose it: press PLAY AGAIN, the card hides itself, but any path back to the lobby that does not run a round leaves it up. Same class of bug the CountText comment at KenopsiaClient:2711-2717 already documents for a different sibling. — LIVE KenopsiaClient:691-703 — hideAll() sets Visible=false on exactly seven frames (selection, info, statusScreen, roundCard, briefing, scoreScreen, announceScreen) and bumps DitherSeq. EndCard is a sibling under `machine` (mirror:3240 "card.Parent = machine", :3238 "card.ZIndex = 94"). Its only dismissal is LIVE:4119-4120 "elseif p.kind == \"round\" or p.kind == \"rolecard\" or p.kind == \"count\" then / if card then card.Visible = false end" — none of which fire on a return to the lobby (selection/info).
- **SettingsPanel row Row_SIMFILTER is read by the client but does not exist in the GUI, so the SimFilter setting is unreachable.** — intentional: unclear — the loop is nil-guarded ("every loop below guards on r.frame", :3001) so it degrades cleanly, and the attribute KenopsiaSimFilter is still read elsewhere. But a player can never toggle it. — studio-src/.../KenopsiaClient.client.luau:2998 — "{ frame = settingsPanel:FindFirstChild(\"Row_SIMFILTER\"), key = \"SimFilter\" },". [RUNTIME] (live-gui.md §5) the complete GuiButton inventory of StarterGui lists Row_UISOUND, Row_MUSIC, Row_REDUCEFLICKER, Row_CRT, Row_SHAKE and Btn_CLOSE — no Row_SIMFILTER.

### Risks

- BOOT COST: every join pays a guaranteed 10 s WaitForChild for a frame that cannot appear, twice. LIVE KenopsiaLoading:254 sits BEFORE the PreloadAsync block, so the bridge holds 10 s longer than intended before it even starts preloading. LIVE KenopsiaClient:3751 is worse — it is a top-level statement, so all 374 lines after it (settings panel wiring, the LobbyError notice at ~4023, the '[Kenopsia] client online' print, and the end-card listener at 4108) are deferred by the full timeout. They run on different scripts so they overlap rather than sum, but both fire every join.
- LOADING SCREEN CAN STRAND WITH NO ENGINE FALLBACK. KenopsiaLoading:12 calls ReplicatedFirst:RemoveDefaultLoadingScreen(), and [RUNTIME] `done` is assigned true at exactly ONE place (LIVE KenopsiaLoading:282, the last line of the second coroutine, weppy search 'done' → 6 hits, one assignment). The ledger coroutine is `while not done do` (LIVE:234). If the second coroutine errors anywhere between line 242 and 282 — and it has NO pcall around the WaitForChilds or the `machineGui.Enabled = true` write — the dots loop runs forever behind a screen nothing will ever destroy. Two unbounded yields sit in that window: `game.Loaded:Wait()` (LIVE:245, no timeout) and `ContentProvider:PreloadAsync` (unbounded; the pcall catches a throw, not a hang).
- THE INVITE SHEET LANDS ON TOP OF THE RESULTS, BY DESIGN. MachineFlow.luau:1271 puts the final board up, :1330 holds 6.0 s (Pacing FinalScore), :1346-1370 fires shiftreport, :1371 holds another 5.0 s. SocialClient:84 calls promptInvite() straight off that packet. So Roblox's invite sheet opens at t+6 s over a standings card that stays on screen for another 5 s — and since nothing renders shiftreport, the popup is the ONLY thing that changes in that window. SocialClient's own header (:12-13) argues this is the honest moment ("the numbers are on screen, everyone is still here"); whether a modal over the verdict is what was wanted is a design call, but it is not an accident and it is not avoidable from the client.
- FIVE SCRIPTS SHARE ONE UNVERSIONED PACKET UNION. MachineState carries 29 kinds with no schema, no version field, and five independent OnClientEvent handlers (KenopsiaClient x2, SocialClient, RunnerClient, SonarClient). Every listener re-tests `p.kind` from scratch. The project has already been bitten by this — KenopsiaClient:318-320 records that `kind = "select"` once cancelled every typewriter mid-word — and the current mitigation is one warn-once per unknown kind (LIVE:3703-3709), which is a log line, not a gate. The mirror being 818 lines behind live on exactly this file is the same failure mode one level up.
- UNGUARDED VECTOR INDEXING IN THE SPECTATE PATH. LIVE KenopsiaClient:3465-3466 — "local pos = Vector3.new(p.pos[1], p.pos[2], p.pos[3]) / local look = Vector3.new(p.look[1], p.look[2], p.look[3])" with no nil check. All six current senders (BirdHunting:1192, Minefield:930/1026/1451, CanteenProtocol:1174, TrialKit:331) do include pos and look, so it is safe today — but a spectate packet from any future trial that omits either field throws inside MachineState.OnClientEvent, and there is no pcall around the dispatch.
- ROJO DOES NOT COVER SIX OF THESE CLIENT FILES. default.project.json maps KenopsiaClient, MachineLayout, MainMenu, UiFx, SimulationGrade, GoreClient, TrialClientKit, Glyphs, the 12 TrialClients and KenopsiaLoading — but NOT SocialClient, AbortScreen, LightGuard, GradeDirector, SonarClient or RunnerClient, all of which exist in the live place. For those six the repo file is not a sync artifact at all; nothing would detect them drifting. Conversely MainMenu and SimulationGrade are mapped but absent from the live place, so a Rojo sync would create them in MainGame.
- THREE SERVICES RACE TO CREATE THE SAME TrialInput RemoteEvent AND ALL THREE BIND OnServerEvent TO IT (Minefield.luau:446-452, CanteenProtocol.luau:914-923, BirdHunting.luau:190-193 "PUSH rides the SAME TrialInput remote Minefield owns a handler"). Every client packet on that remote is therefore delivered to all three handlers, and each must self-filter on trialId. Combined with Envelope being unwired, the client→server direction has no shared validation at all.

### Uncertainties

- Everything a play test would show. All [RUNTIME] evidence here is from the EDIT-mode DataModel via weppy (studioState "edit") — live source reads and instance queries, not a running client. Nothing was played. In particular I did not observe the 10 s stalls, the unknown-kind warns, or the invite sheet actually firing.
- Whether a `Warning` frame appears in PlayerGui at runtime. Nothing in the live place's 115 scripts creates one (and hideAll/MachineLayout only ever read it), so I believe the timeout is guaranteed — but the settling observation is: start a play test, watch Players.<name>.PlayerGui.KenopsiaMachine for a `Warning` child, and time the gap between KenopsiaLoading's start and its gui:Destroy(). If Warning never appears, both waits burn their full 10 s.
- The exact live line numbering of MachineLayout and KenopsiaLoading outside the regions I read. Live MachineLayout is 728 lines vs the mirror's 695 (+33) and live KenopsiaLoading is 286 vs 163 (+123) — I confirmed the load-bearing parts of both live (all three KenopsiaLoading WaitForChild calls, the whole tail from line 240, and MachineLayout's zero EndCard references) but the +33 / +123 unread lines could contain behaviour I did not see. Every MachineLayout quote in this map is from the MIRROR unless marked LIVE.
- Whether the live `Overlay` and `K.Theme` abstractions (LIVE KenopsiaClient:3683 "Overlay.show(...)", :3689 "Overlay.showHud(p)", :4105 "K.Theme.layout(machine)") change any conclusion about the feed/hud/cover rendering. They exist only live and I read only their call sites, not their definitions. This does not affect the set difference — the kinds are handled either way — but it does mean I cannot describe what `feed` and `hud` actually draw.
- Whether Kenopsia_DEV has its own return-to-MainGame or back-out affordance. I confirmed the DEV→MainGame teleport (dev-src PlaylistService.luau:166-169) from disk only; the DEV place (129909297895850) was not connected this session. A grep of dev-src found no MainGame→DEV counterpart, but I could not query that place's live GUI.
- Byte-level parity for the files I quoted from the mirror only — AbortScreen, LightGuard, GoreClient, RunnerClient, SonarClient, TrialClientKit, TrialClients/*, Envelope, and the server services. Line counts matched live for Envelope's neighbours and for MachineFlow/Profiles/RoomService/Contexts/Telemetry, and my live `kind = "` census over all 34 server scripts matched the mirror exactly (88 hits vs 86 + the two live-only FieldPush/-adjacent lines), which is strong corroboration — but no byte diff was performed on any file.
- Why the mirror is missing the live `feed`, `hud` and `fieldPush` handlers plus the whole FieldPush service. That is real functionality living only in the place file, with no repo copy and (for FieldPush) no Rojo mapping to bring it back. Whether that is unpushed work or an intentional live-only experiment, I cannot tell from source.

## Kenopsia MainGame (placeId 110672791536316) — the three ENABLED trials (BIRD HUNTING / DEAD ZONE / CANTEEN PROTOCOL) and the trial framework (MachineFlow registry, TrialKit, Contexts, Playlist, TrialRules, Scoring, Pacing)

The trial registry is a two-gate system: an id must appear in GameConfig.Playlist.TrialIds AND its MachineFlow TRIALS entry must declare ready = true; 3 of 15 pass both gates today (birdhunt, minefield, canteen), and I confirmed the same 3-true/12-false pattern in the live place. [SOURCE + RUNTIME] The framework has split in two: TrialKit's Round object (place/countdown/tick/kill/scores/endRound/cleanup) is the contract for the twelve MP-05 trials, but NONE of the three enabled trials require it — BirdHunting, Minefield and CanteenProtocol each hand-roll their own livingOf/announce/countdown/scoring, so the shared framework is exercised in production only by disabled stubs. Round lifecycle in TrialKit is newRound (registry + guard armed before any yield) -> place (freeze at spawn, mark bodyless members done) -> begin -> countdown (3-2-1, opens input, arms deadline) -> tick (0.05 s loop bounded by deadline and a +20 s failsafe) -> scores -> endRound (cover + 0.6 s) -> cleanup (idempotent, LIFO, unregister-first, restore humanoids, teleport home). Bird Hunting's hunter is picked in MachineFlow, not in BirdHunting: `room.members[(roundIndex - 1) % #room.members + 1]`, always starting at index 1, over exactly 3 legs for every player count — so with 4 players the fourth roster slot (deterministically the 4th player to join, since members is append-order) NEVER hunts, and Bird Hunting is played at most once per session. There is NO per-trial teaching text triggered for a first-time player: the only per-trial instruction is the CONTROLS window inside the lobby Info panel (MachineLayout.TRIAL_TEXT), plus a 5-line status card of the trial name + tagline; the two-page BRIEFING screen is an opt-in main-menu panel that no trial flow ever opens. Bands are declared once in TrialRules (FINISHED 2000 / ALIVE 1000 / OUT 0, detail clamped 0..999) but no enabled trial calls TrialRules.key: Canteen re-implements the bands correctly with its own DETAIL_CAP 999, Bird Hunting uses no bands at all (raw points), and Minefield's escape branch produces detail 1000 — 1001 with the clean-run tiebreaker — which exceeds 999. Live-vs-repo drift is worse than briefed: the live place contains a Services module (FieldPush, 331 lines) that does not exist in the repo at all, and live BirdHunting and Minefield both require it. [RUNTIME]

### Unfinished / dormant

- **TrialKit — the declared trial framework — is not used by any ENABLED trial. All three shipping trials (BirdHunting, Minefield, CanteenProtocol) hand-roll livingOf / announce / countdown / cleanup / scoring. TrialKit's Round object is exercised in production only by the 12 DISABLED modules, 11 of which are no-op stubs.** — intentional: yes, by history but not by design — CanteenProtocol.luau:82 says it verbatim: `-- present. The canteen predates TrialKit and must keep running without it, so`. The consequence (the framework is unproven against live traffic; the two code paths can drift) does not appear to be a decision anyone recorded. — [SOURCE] grep for `require(script.Parent.TrialKit)` across .../Services/ returns only ArmsIssue:16, AudienceCam:17, Breather:16, Carrier:16, Clearance:16, ClearTheDeck:16, Crawler:16, CutToSpec:16, FloorCheck:16, MachineFlow:26, PalletDuty:16, Podium:17, Ricochet:16, SortingFloor:15, Upstream:16. BirdHunting.luau's require block is lines 7-12 (BloodFX/Pacing/Feel only); Minefield's is 21-25; CanteenProtocol's soft-loads it in a pcall at :85-:90 `local TrialKit = nil` ... `if ok and type(kit) == "table" then TrialKit = kit end` for a palette colour.
- **With 4 players, roster slot 4 never receives the hunter role in BIRD HUNTING, and the trial is played at most once per session.** — intentional: no. BirdHunting.luau:452 states the opposite intent: `-- Every numbered round gives every participant one Hunter leg.` That invariant held when legs were 4 for a 4-player session; the 22.08.2026 cut to 3 legs (Pacing.luau:58-59) broke it and the comment was never updated. Two other comments are stale the same way: Pacing.luau:55 `-- 3-player sessions run 3 and 4-player sessions run 4` and MachineFlow.luau:1102 `-- (2p 4 / 3p 3 / 4p 4)` — both contradict LEGS = {3,3,3} directly above/below them. — [SOURCE+RUNTIME] .../Services/MachineFlow.luau:1023 `hunter = room.members[(roundIndex - 1) % #room.members + 1]` (identical at line 1023 of the LIVE MachineFlow), driven by :988 `local rounds = Pacing.roundsFor(trial.id, playerCount)` and .../Rules/Pacing.luau:60 `local LEGS = { [2] = 3, [3] = 3, [4] = 3 }` (identical at live line 60). members is append-on-join: .../Services/RoomService.luau:172 `table.insert(room.members, {`.
- **No per-trial teaching or first-time briefing exists. Nothing in the shipped tree keys off whether a player has played before — no firstTime/hasPlayed/tutorial/onboarding flag anywhere.** — intentional: unclear. MenuConfig.luau:236 records that the Briefing GUI was found orphaned — `-- Wakes KenopsiaMachine.Briefing, which is fully authored in the place and shown by nothing (found 25.08.2026).` — i.e. the panel was authored and then not wired for months. Nothing says a trial-time briefing was ever planned. — [SOURCE] case-insensitive grep for `firsttime|first_time|hasplayed|newplayer|onboard|tutorial|seenbriefing` across studio-src/ returns zero matches. The only per-trial instruction surface is MachineLayout's TRIAL_TEXT (.../MachineLayout.client.luau:132 `local TRIAL_TEXT = {`), written into the lobby Info panel; the only prose that explains the trials is the opt-in main-menu BRIEFING (.../Config/MenuConfig.luau:237).
- **Every TRIALS registry entry declares `subtitle` and `showInterRoundScore`; neither field is read anywhere in the shipped tree.** — intentional: no — these read as leftovers from a card design that was replaced by the `tagline`/status-card path. — [SOURCE] grep `subtitle` across studio-src/ returns 15 hits, all assignments in MachineFlow.luau (:84, :96, :120, :142, :152, :162, :172, :182, :192, :202, :212, :222, :232, :242, :252) plus one unrelated MenuConfig.luau:56 `SubGap`. grep `showInterRoundScore` returns 15 hits, all inside MachineFlow.luau, all assignments. So e.g. BIRD HUNTING's `subtitle = "THE SIGHTLINE NEVER BLINKS._"` is never shown to anyone.
- **Shared/Net/Envelope.luau has an offline proof and no production consumer; Contexts.guardedRun and RoundContext:envelopeExpectation likewise.** — intentional: unclear — Contexts.luau:11-21 describes the envelope/token design as load-bearing, but the three shipping trials each validate tokens with their own inline ladder instead (TrialKit.luau:398-402, Minefield.luau:452ff, CanteenProtocol.luau:923ff, BirdHunting.luau:213-215). — [SOURCE] grep `Envelope` across studio-src/ finds only two passing mentions in comments (.../Rules/MachineCam.luau:9 and :80) — no require. tests/envelope.lua:17 loads it: `local Envelope = loadModule("studio-src/ReplicatedStorage/Kenopsia/Shared/Net/Envelope.luau")`. `guardedRun` appears only at .../Services/Contexts.luau:236 and tests/contexts.lua:167/:174. `envelopeExpectation` appears only at .../Services/Contexts.luau:120.
- **SortingFloor is fully implemented and passes QA but is held out of the registry; the other eleven MP-05 trials are the same 2.9K stub file.** — intentional: yes — the comment at MachineFlow.luau:254 names the decision and its author. — [SOURCE] .../Services/MachineFlow.luau:254 `ready = false, -- built and verified 23.08.2026; held back until after release (user call: three trials first)`. File sizes: SortingFloor.luau 11.6K vs ArmsIssue/Breather/Carrier/Clearance/ClearTheDeck/Crawler/CutToSpec/FloorCheck/PalletDuty/Ricochet/Upstream all exactly 2.9K, and CutToSpec.luau:2 reads `-- CUT TO SPEC -- FRAMEWORK STUB (MP-05 D13).`
- **The repo mirror studio-src/ is behind the live place for MORE files than the briefed list: a whole Services module, FieldPush (331 lines), exists live and has no file in the repo, and live BirdHunting and Minefield both require it.** — intentional: unclear — nothing in the repo mentions FieldPush at all, so this is either unpushed work or a Studio-side edit that was never mirrored. — [RUNTIME] weppy query_instances children of game.ServerScriptService.KenopsiaServer.Services returns 33 modules including FieldPush; the repo directory has 32 .luau files and no FieldPush. Live FieldPush line 2: `-- Shared, round-scoped Headbutt authority for Bird Hunting and Dead Zone.` Live BirdHunting:8 and live Minefield:22 both `require(script.Parent.FieldPush)`.
- **A user-initiated match abort that lands mid-round is reported to players as an engine failure.** — intentional: no — the abort branch was added on 30.08.2026 (comment at :760-766) and the two call sites were not widened to accept its new return value. — [SOURCE] .../Services/MachineFlow.luau:782 `return false, room.aborting and "aborted" or "timeout"`, but the call sites only special-case "timeout": :1111 `if legScores == "timeout" then timedOut = true else error(legScores, 0) end` (and identically at :1118). The thrown "aborted" reaches :1385 `outcome = "error"` and then :1398 `RoomServiceRef.abort(room, "MINIGAME ERROR")`.

### Risks

- HUNTER ROTATION ARITHMETIC (the headline finding). The hunter is chosen in MachineFlow, not in BirdHunting: [SOURCE+RUNTIME] MachineFlow.luau:1023 `hunter = room.members[(roundIndex - 1) % #room.members + 1]` — confirmed byte-identical at line 1023 of the LIVE MachineFlow. It ALWAYS starts from member index 1 (roundIndex begins at 1, so the first term is 0 % n + 1 = 1). Leg count is fixed at 3 for every player count: Pacing.luau:60 `local LEGS = { [2] = 3, [3] = 3, [4] = 3 }` (confirmed live at line 60), reached via MachineFlow.luau:988 `local rounds = Pacing.roundsFor(trial.id, playerCount)` and Pacing.luau:63-64 `if trialId == "birdhunt" then return row(LEGS, playerCount)`. Working it out: 2 PLAYERS (n=2): leg1 -> (1-1)%2+1 = 1; leg2 -> (2-1)%2+1 = 2; leg3 -> (3-1)%2+1 = 1. Members [1,2,1] — member 1 hunts TWICE, member 2 once. Nobody denied, but the split is 2:1. 3 PLAYERS (n=3): leg1 -> 0%3+1 = 1; leg2 -> 1%3+1 = 2; leg3 -> 2%3+1 = 3. Members [1,2,3] — exactly one hunt each, the only fair case. 4 PLAYERS (n=4): leg1 -> 0%4+1 = 1; leg2 -> 1%4+1 = 2; leg3 -> 2%4+1 = 3. Members [1,2,3] — MEMBER 4 IS NEVER THE HUNTER. There is no second chance: Playlist.order permutes each id exactly once (Playlist.luau:53 `-- Fisher-Yates. Every id exactly once, no repeats`), so BIRD HUNTING runs at most once per session. And the denied slot is not random: room.members is append-on-join and never shuffled (RoomService.luau:44 `members = {},        -- ordered: { userId, displayName, ready }`; :172 `table.insert(room.members, {`), so it is deterministically the 4th player to sit down. BirdHunting's own fallback rotation (BirdHunting.luau:480-481, live 481-482) is identical in shape and only reached on a direct/solo call.
- SCORE CONSEQUENCE OF THE DENIED SLOT. A hunter leg is worth far more than a runner leg, so slot 4's ceiling is roughly half everyone else's. Hunter: [SOURCE] BirdHunting.luau:1518 `scores[uid] = math.min(st.points, DETAIL_CAP)` with DETAIL_CAP 999 (:92) and a stated legit maximum of 745 (:91 `-- 3 x 115 (345) + Birds x BirdDetail (400) = 745`). Runner: :1520 `scores[uid] = 100 + math.floor((st.escapeTimeLeft or 0) * 2)` where escapeTimeLeft <= ROUND_TIME = 60 (:1389, :29), so <= 220 per leg; a timed-out runner gets :1522 `scores[uid] = math.floor(st.progress * 100)` (<= 100) and a dead runner :1524 `scores[uid] = 0`. Three legs: members 1-3 can reach ~745 + 2x220 = 1185; member 4 can reach at most 3x220 = 660. Those totals are what MachineFlow ranks (:1149 `trialTotals[uid] = (trialTotals[uid] or 0) + value`, :1182-1185), so the 4th-to-join player is structurally placed lower in BIRD HUNTING's 1700-point distribution.
- DETAIL POINTS CAN EXCEED 999 — in DEAD ZONE, and only there. The framework contract is TrialRules.luau:29-37: `TrialRules.BAND = { FINISHED = 2000, ALIVE = 1000, OUT = 0 }`, `TrialRules.BAND_WIDTH = 1000`, and key() clamps: `if d > TrialRules.BAND_WIDTH - 1 then d = TrialRules.BAND_WIDTH - 1 end`. No enabled trial calls it (grep TrialRules.key / TrialKit.key across the three enabled modules: zero hits; the only callers are the MP-05 stubs, e.g. CutToSpec.luau:63). CANTEEN re-implements it correctly — CanteenProtocol.luau:174-176 (`ELIMINATED_BAND = 0` / `ALIVE_BAND = 1000` / `FINISHED_BAND = 2000`), :182 `local DETAIL_CAP = 999`, and all three branches clamp (:1343 `key = FINISHED_BAND + math.min(DETAIL_CAP, math.floor(spare * FINISH_SPEED_STEP))`, :1345, :1349) — max key 2999, detail never above 999. BIRD HUNTING uses no bands at all: its four score branches (:1518-1524) are raw numbers, so 'FINISHED > ALIVE > OUT' is not enforced there — a timed-out runner at full progress (100) outranks a hunter who hit nothing (0). DEAD ZONE BREAKS THE 999 RULE: Minefield.luau:1513-1515 `local ESCAPE_BAND = 2000` / `local ORDER_STEP = 100` / `local DETAIL_CAP = 999`, then :1522-1523 `sc = ESCAPE_BAND + math.max(0, 1000 - ORDER_STEP * ((ps.escapeOrder or 1) - 1))` — the FIRST escapee gets detail exactly 1000, and DETAIL_CAP is applied only on the eliminated branch (:1527 `sc = math.min(detail, DETAIL_CAP)`). Then :1531 `if not ps.usedScanner then sc += 1 end` runs for EVERYONE including escapees, so the true maximum is 2000 + 1000 + 1 = 3001, i.e. detail 1001. Inside Dead Zone alone nothing is corrupted (there is no band above 2000), and the comment at :1499 half-acknowledges it (`escaped       2000 + 1000 down to 2000`) — but the +1 pushing it past the band width is not acknowledged anywhere, and any future trial that stacks a band above 2000 while reusing this pattern would silently cross it.
- SUMMING ORDERING KEYS ACROSS ROUNDS is arithmetically unsafe and is what the session actually does. MachineFlow.luau:1149 `trialTotals[uid] = (trialTotals[uid] or 0) + value` adds banded keys, then :1182-1183 ranks on `a.raw > b.raw`. CanteenProtocol.luau:158-159 states the design (`runRound returns one ordering key per player and MachineFlow sums those across rounds`), but summing bands is not order-preserving: in a 3-round DEAD ZONE, escaping SECOND three times (2900+1 each = 8703) beats escaping FIRST twice and dying once (3001+3001+1 = 6003). Consistency beats peaks by construction — defensible as a design, but it is nowhere written down as one, and no test covers it.
- TRIALINPUT IS ONE REMOTE WITH FOUR INDEPENDENT SERVER HANDLERS, each with its own hand-written validation ladder: TrialKit.luau:387 (12-step, the strict one), Minefield.luau:452, CanteenProtocol.luau:923, BirdHunting.luau:196. Only TrialKit's ladder enforces per-player/per-action rate limiting (:420 `if now < (ps.lastInput[action] or 0) + (sp.perAction[action] or sp.cooldown) then return end`) and the NaN-seq guard (:413 `if payload.seq ~= payload.seq then return end`); the three enabled trials each re-derive their own subset (BirdHunting's is only :213-216: active / phase / roundToken). Any future hardening of the ladder has to be applied four times or it is not applied at all.
- A PLAYER WITH NO LIVING CHARACTER is handled four different ways. TrialKit (disabled trials): Round:place marks them done and gives no feedback at all — TrialKit.luau:588-589 `if not s then` / `if ps then ps.done = true end`; they are then skipped by countdown (:562-563) and by kill/out (:733, :762), and Round:scores zero-fills over room.members (:775-779). DEAD ZONE: Minefield.luau:760 `state[mm.userId] = { done = s == nil, escaped = false, progress = 0, usedScanner = false, ...}` — done, progress 0, and then :1531 `if not ps.usedScanner then sc += 1 end` awards them the clean-run tiebreaker, so a player who was never in the round scores 1 and outranks a live player who used the sonar and made no progress (0). CANTEEN: CanteenProtocol.luau:1099 `finished = false, finishing = false, eliminated = s == nil,` -> scored 0 (:1349 with eliminatedAt nil -> survived 0). BIRD HUNTING: the state entry is created with `done = isSniper` (BirdHunting.luau:664), so a bodyless RUNNER is not done at creation and is only dropped on the first main-loop tick (:1446-1449 `elseif not st.sniper then` / `st.done = true`). MachineFlow mitigates all of this by force-respawning before every round (:1076-1081 `pcall(function() plr:LoadCharacter() end)`) and waiting — but the wait uses a key that does not exist: :1082 `local deadline = os.clock() + (Pacing.Timing.RespawnGrace or 5)` and Pacing.Timing (Pacing.luau:120-183) has no RespawnGrace field, so this is a silent constant 5, not a tunable.
- THE ONLY PER-TRIAL INSTRUCTION IS VISIBLE FOR ~3.5 SECONDS BETWEEN TRIALS. The CONTROLS window lives inside the lobby Info panel (MachineLayout.client.luau:333 `local cw = info and info:FindFirstChild("ControlsWindow")`; confirmed live at game.StarterGui.KenopsiaMachine.Info.ControlsWindow), and Info is shown only by showInfo (KenopsiaClient.client.luau:2199 `info.Visible = true`), which every other card's hideAll() closes. In the lobby it stays up until READY is pressed (preFlow sends the info packet at MachineFlow.luau:481-486 and then waits for readiness) — so the FIRST trial's controls can be read at leisure. Between trials it is up for exactly Pacing.Timing.Reveal = 3.5 s (MachineFlow.luau:963-965; Pacing.luau:121), competing with a typewriter animation, and the 8-second BRIEFING card that follows (:969-976, ControlCard = 8.0) shows the status screen, which does NOT contain the controls window. So trials 2 and 3 are effectively unbriefed.

### Uncertainties

- EXACT VERBATIM TEXT A PLAYER IS TOLD BEFORE EACH TRIAL — server side is certain, client rendering is not. From source, the sequence per trial is: (1) roulette card {kind='selection', name=<displayName>}; (2) info card 'NEXT SIMULATION: <displayName>' (KenopsiaClient.client.luau:2214 `typewrite(info.NextLabel, "NEXT SIMULATION: " .. (p.name or "?"), ...)`) with the CONTROLS window beside it; (3) the BRIEFING status card, MachineFlow.luau:969-975, five lines: `trial.displayName`, `trial.tagline`, `" "`, `isFinal and "FINAL AUDIT. DOUBLE WEIGHT." or ("Minigame %d of %d."):format(trialIndex, #order)`, `"Bootstrapping simulation ..."`; (4) the round card {kind='round', verbs = trial.tagline} (:1011-1012); (5) Bird Hunting only, the role card '<NAME> IS THE HUNTER.' (:1024-1026 + MachineVoice.luau:126); (6) the trial's own one-line announce. VERBATIM PER TRIAL — BIRD HUNTING: displayName 'BIRD HUNTING' (MachineFlow.luau:82), tagline 'CROSS THE FIELD. THE TOWER IS LOADED.' (:87), unused subtitle 'THE SIGHTLINE NEVER BLINKS._' (:84), controls sec1 'RUNNERS:' sec2 'HUNTER:' with Desktop rows 'Move  [{Move}]' / 'Crouch [{Crouch}]' / 'Aim + Shoot [{Fire}]' / 'Scope [{Scope}]  x2 / x4 [{Zoom}]' and scoring page 'Headshot     instant  +100' / 'Body shot    2 to drop' / 'Winged       +15 / kill +60' / 'Escape       +100 & time' / 'Timeout      distance only' (MachineLayout.client.luau:133-162); in-trial announce SKIPPED in every real session (BirdHunting.luau:644 `if not forcedHunterUserId then`). DEAD ZONE: displayName 'DEAD ZONE' (:94), tagline 'MARK THE GROUND. OUTRUN THE PRESS.' (:99), unused subtitle 'READ THE FLOOR. KEEP MOVING._' (:96), controls sec1 'RUNNERS:' sec2 'SONAR:' rows 'Move  [{Move}]' / 'Use sonar [HOLD {Scan}]' with an EMPTY scoring page `scoring = {}` (MachineLayout.client.luau:164-187), in-trial announce 'MARK THE GROUND. OUTRUN THE PRESS.' (Minefield.luau:658 — the same sentence as the tagline, so the player is told one thing twice and the mine mechanic never). CANTEEN PROTOCOL: displayName 'CANTEEN PROTOCOL' (:118), tagline 'FREEZE WHEN THE INSPECTOR LOOKS.' (:121), unused subtitle 'RATION ISSUED. EAT ONLY WHEN UNOBSERVED._' (:120), controls sec1 'DINERS:' sec2 'OBSERVER:' rows 'Seated - no moving' / 'Load fork [{Load}]' / 'Swallow [{Swallow}]' / 'Never swallow while watched' plus a second page 'Plate         16 rations' / 'Fork          holds 4' / 'Loading       always safe' / 'Swallowing    only unobserved' / 'Finish first  ranks highest' (MachineLayout.client.luau:195-225), in-trial announce 'EAT EVERYTHING. DO NOT BE SEEN.' (CanteenProtocol.luau:1198). NEEDS_RUNTIME: the KenopsiaClient repo copy is known-stale, so whether these strings actually render (and for how long) must be settled by a play test — the observation that would settle it is a 4-player play test with a screen recording of the Info panel from the {kind='info'} packet to the {kind='status'} briefing card, checking that ControlsWindow.Row1..Row3 are populated and legible and that Page2 is reachable for canteen.
- DEAD ZONE's scoring page is empty (`scoring = {}`, MachineLayout.client.luau:187) while Bird Hunting and Canteen both have five rows. That is consistent with Page2/PageLabel being hidden for minefield (KenopsiaClient.client.luau:3074 gates the second page on `TrialId == "canteen"` only), but whether the client leaves stale rows on screen from a previous trial is NEEDS_RUNTIME — applyControlsText only writes `trial.scoring[i] or ""` into p2.R1..R5 (:1092), which should blank them, but p2.Visible is owned by KenopsiaClient's renderPage and the repo copy is stale.
- BirdHunting.luau:1450-1451 (`else` / `allDone = false`) looks unreachable: the sniper's state is created with `done = isSniper` (:664), so the sniper never enters the `if not st.done` block at :1419. I did not find a reset of st.done for a sniper, but I did not read the whole 1582-line loop, so I am not asserting it is dead. NEEDS_RUNTIME/further read: grep the live BirdHunting for every write to `.done` on a sniper state entry.
- I could not verify the live KenopsiaClient, PS1Animate, Minefield-client, BloodFX, GoreClient or AnimationIds against the repo — the briefing names them as stale and my runtime access this session was read-only script search, which I used on the server modules. Everything I attribute to the client above is [SOURCE] from a mirror that is known to be behind. The extra finding is that the staleness is not limited to client scripts: live FieldPush (331 lines) has no repo file at all, and live BirdHunting/Minefield require it, so those two SERVER modules are behind live too. Live MachineFlow and live Pacing, by contrast, matched the repo line-for-line at every point I probed (registry ready flags at 88/100/130 and twelve false; hunter pick at 1023; LEGS at 60).
- The 'skipped, never substituted' path for a departed hunter (BirdHunting.luau:462-476) returns a fully zeroed score table for that leg. Whether a 4-player room can actually lose a member mid-session without RoomService aborting the match first (min 2, GameConfig.luau:22 `MinPlayers = 2`) I did not trace, so I cannot say how often that path runs. NEEDS_RUNTIME: a 3-player session where one player leaves during Bird Hunting, checking the console for `[BH] leg %s skipped: scheduled hunter %d has left`.
- Studio was connected in EDIT mode only (weppy system_info: `"studioState": "edit"`), and this was a read-only pass, so nothing here is backed by an actual play test. Every claim labelled [RUNTIME] above is a live SOURCE READ of the connected place's scripts and instance tree, not observed behaviour. No console log or play test evidence is included.

## Analytics + telemetry honesty — Services/Telemetry.luau, all Telemetry call sites in studio-src/, StarterPlayerScripts/LightGuard.client.luau (repo root: C:/Users/Asus/Claude/Kenopsia_Roblox Project)

The entire analytics surface of Kenopsia is ONE module (Services/Telemetry.luau) wrapping two engine calls, five funnel-step firings, and exactly one custom event. [RUNTIME] weppy manage_scripts search over the live MainGame place (placeId 110672791536316, 115 scripts searched) found "AnalyticsService" in exactly one script — Telemetry — at lines 4/17/93/104, and "TelemetryEvent" in exactly two scripts (LightGuard:60, Telemetry:9/136/139); live line numbers match the repo mirror byte-for-byte, so the KNOWN HAZARD does not apply to this area. The funnel is "KenopsiaRetention": 1 Joined (Profiles.luau:261), 2 RouletteSeen (MachineFlow.luau:476), 3 Round1Done (MachineFlow.luau:1156), 4 VerdictSeen (MachineFlow.luau:1327), 5 RematchAccepted (MachineFlow.luau:505). There is ONE custom event, "MobileBlackScreen", and no server code ever calls Telemetry.event — the only path to LogCustomEvent is the client remote (Telemetry.luau:128), whitelisted to that single name (Telemetry.luau:27-29). Three named metrics are dishonest as written: "Joined" is gated behind the DataStore load and its retry backoff, so a player who bounces in the first ~7-95 s produces NO event at all — the exact bounce cohort the MASTERPLAN names as the primary ranking signal; "RematchAccepted" is not acceptance but "was a verdict participant, is still seated 6.4 s into the next lobby roulette, and did not explicitly press un-ready", and it is structurally SKIPPED for the players who accept fastest; "MobileBlackScreen" is a device+quality heuristic (touch device AND SavedQualityLevel <= 4, where 0/Automatic is the mobile default) with no pixel evidence whatsoever. There is NO per-match session identifier anywhere in analytics: LogFunnelStepEvent's funnelSessionId argument is hard-coded nil (Telemetry.luau:104) even though RoomService mints a real room.sessionId at RoomService.luau:297. The menu place Kenopsia_DEV (129909297895850, dev-src/) — the actual front door, where parties form before teleporting into a reserved MainGame server — has zero telemetry of any kind. The plan document promises a 6-step funnel and 6 custom events; 5 of the 6 events and 1 of the 6 steps do not exist in code.

### Unfinished / dormant

- **Funnel step 'FirstInput' is documented but does not exist. The funnel therefore cannot measure the one thing MASTERPLAN:37 names as fatal ('>60 s before first input').** — intentional: unclear — the doc is written in the present tense as if shipped, and no TODO/parked marker exists next to it. The step numbering 1..5 with no gap suggests FirstInput was dropped rather than deferred, but nothing in code says so. — docs/INNER-GAME-PLAN.md:245 "funnel `Joined -> FirstInput -> RouletteSeen -> ...`" vs grep 'FirstInput' across studio-src + dev-src = 0 matches. Implemented steps are only 1,2,3,4,5 (Profiles.luau:261; MachineFlow.luau:476, 1156, 1327, 505).
- **Five of the six promised custom events do not exist: TrialCompleted, SessionVerdict, QuestClaimed, StreakTick, EmotePlayed. Telemetry.event is never called by any server module.** — intentional: no — Telemetry.luau:2 calls itself an 'analytics skeleton (G1)' and :86 exposes a public Telemetry.event with a full customFields folder (lines 43-66) that nothing uses. The plumbing was built and never wired. — docs/INNER-GAME-PLAN.md:246-248 lists all six; grep across studio-src + dev-src returns 0 matches for the first five. The only call to Telemetry.event is Telemetry.luau:128, inside onClientReport, gated by CLIENT_EVENT_WHITELIST (Telemetry.luau:27-29) to MobileBlackScreen alone.
- **No session/leave/quit event of any kind. Session length, voluntary quit, involuntary solo-exit and mid-match abort are all unmeasured.** — intentional: no — MASTERPLAN.md:291 requires 'bounce < 60 s' and play days, neither of which is derivable without a session boundary. — Profiles.luau:274-283 (onLeave) contains no Telemetry call; SoloExit.luau:88-110 (sendToMenu) contains no Telemetry call; RoomService.luau:320-345 (removeMember, including abortMatch 'PLAYER LEFT - CANNOT CONTINUE WITH A SINGLE PLAYER' at :340) contains no Telemetry call.
- **funnelSessionId is hard-coded nil, so repeat sessions by the same player are conflated/deduped even though a real session id is minted one module away.** — intentional: yes, and documented — Telemetry.luau:8 "(funnelSessionId nil: documented-optional, fine for one-time funnels)" and MachineFlow.luau:473 "One-time funnel -- Analytics dedupes repeats per player." The choice is deliberate; the consequence (no per-match measurement) is nowhere acknowledged. — Telemetry.luau:104 "AnalyticsService:LogFunnelStepEvent(player, funnelName, nil, step, stepName)" ([RUNTIME] confirmed verbatim in the live place); Telemetry.luau:100 signature has no session parameter; RoomService.luau:297 "room.sessionId = (\"S%d-%d\"):format(...)" exists and is unused by analytics.
- **The menu place Kenopsia_DEV (129909297895850) — where players actually land, form parties and start matchmaking — has zero telemetry.** — intentional: unclear — the menu place is a later addition (kenopsia-dev.project.json servePlaceIds) and Telemetry predates it, but nothing in docs notes the gap. The effect is that 'Joined' in MainGame is a post-teleport second join, not a first touch. — dev-src/ServerScriptService/KenopsiaServer/Main.server.luau:5-7 requires only RoomService, PlaylistService, MenuPresenceService; kenopsia-dev.project.json:26-35 maps no Telemetry module; grep 'Telemetry|Analytics|TelemetryEvent' across dev-src = 0 matches; dev-src/StarterPlayer/StarterPlayerScripts contains no LightGuard.
- **Step 2 'RouletteSeen' can fire for a player who structurally cannot reach step 3, producing a permanent artificial drop between steps 2 and 3.** — intentional: no — the comment at MachineFlow.luau:472-473 frames step 2 as 'the first thing a seated player watches' with no awareness of the solo case. — MachineFlow.luau:1498 "if #room.members > 0 and not room.preShown then" spawns preFlow for a single seated player; RouletteSeen fires at :476 unconditionally for every member; but RoomService.luau:314 "if #room.members < minPlayers() then return end" with GameConfig.luau:22 "MinPlayers = 2" means a solo player can never start a match.
- **Step ordering is violated for step 5: RematchAccepted (5) fires ~6.4 s AFTER the next session's RouletteSeen (2), in the same preFlow call.** — intentional: no — the comment block at :490-498 discusses only who counts, never when. Placing the step-5 emit after the step-2 emit of the following session appears to be a side effect of reusing preFlow as the delivery point. — MachineFlow.luau:476 fires step 2; :479 "task.wait(Pacing.Timing.LobbyReveal or 6.4)" (Pacing.luau:156 "LobbyReveal  = 6.4"); :505 fires step 5.

### Risks

- 'Joined' HIDES EARLY EXITS — PROVEN FROM SOURCE. Telemetry.funnel(plr, FUNNEL, 1, "Joined") sits at Profiles.luau:261, which is reached only after the DataStore load loop (Profiles.luau:227-258). Three guards return without firing it: :232 (+:239 return) 'left while we were loading', :252 after a backoff wait, and :258 immediately before the fire. With LOAD_ATTEMPTS=3 and LOAD_BACKOFF={2,5} (Profiles.luau:42-43) a failing or throttled store costs at minimum 7 s of task.wait plus three UpdateAsync round trips; a 'locked by another server' result (Profiles.luau:124 "return nil, \"locked by another server\"") is treated as a plain failure and runs the full retry path even though it will never succeed within LOCK_STALE_SECONDS=180. Meanwhile the boot watchdog holds joiners out for 7 s (MachineFlow.luau:1455 "if os.clock() - plr:GetAttribute(\"KenopsiaJoinedAt\") > 7") and Profiles.GREETING_DELAY=8 (Profiles.luau:48) — i.e. the first ~8 s are a black content-warning screen. A player who bounces in exactly that window produces NO funnel event at all. The published bounce number will therefore be systematically optimistic, and MASTERPLAN.md:291's 'bounce < 60 s' gate is not measurable by this instrumentation.
- 'RematchAccepted' MEASURES SEAT OCCUPANCY, NOT ACCEPTANCE — and is INVERTED for the fastest accepters. room.rematch enrolls every verdict participant unconditionally (MachineFlow.luau:1279-1281 "for _, uid in ctx.participantUserIds do rematchIds[uid] = true end" / "room.rematch = { userIds = rematchIds, until_ = os.clock() + 60 }"). The only removal is an explicit un-ready press (RoomService.luau:365-366). The fire at MachineFlow.luau:505 is gated on :501 "if rematch and os.clock() < rematch.until_ then" and :503 "if rematch.userIds[m.userId] then" — i.e. still enrolled, still a member, inside 60 s. The code's own comment admits it: MachineFlow.luau:496-497 "Das Funnel feuert weiterhin: wer nach dem Verdikt sitzen geblieben ist, zaehlt als Rematch-Interesse" ('whoever stayed seated after the verdict counts as rematch interest'). An AFK player counts as an acceptance. Worse: since 30.08.2026 removed carryReady, acceptance is a manual READY press, and if all seated members press READY during the 6.4 s LobbyReveal wait, RoomService.luau:278 sets phase='Starting' and MachineFlow.luau:480 "if room.phase ~= \"Waiting\" or #room.members == 0 then return end" returns before line 505 — the players who most enthusiastically accepted the rematch are the ones EXCLUDED from the metric. The step name is the opposite of the behaviour.
- 'MobileBlackScreen' IS A MOBILE-PLAYER COUNTER, NOT A BLACK-SCREEN COUNTER. The two conditions are LightGuard.client.luau:33 "if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then return end" and :39 "if quality == nil or quality > CONFIG.LowQualityMax then return end" with LowQualityMax=4 (:19) and the explicit note :40 "-- 0 is Automatic, 1..4 the low tiers -- both count as at-risk". SavedQualityLevel=Automatic(0) is the default for any player who never opened settings, so on touch devices the predicate is close to always-true. There is no luminance read, no frame sample, no Lighting inspection — the header says so at :7-9 "it fires on the heuristic, not on a proven black frame; the name is kept for the analytics counter". It also fires AFTER the mitigation is applied (:51-55 raise the ambient floor, :57-69 then report), so even a client that was successfully fixed is counted. The name is misleading and the counter will be read as a bug rate when it is a device census. [RUNTIME] the live LocalScript is byte-identical to the repo file, so this is not a mirror-lag artifact.
- NO PER-MATCH SESSION IDENTIFIER ANYWHERE IN ANALYTICS. Telemetry.luau:104 passes nil for funnelSessionId, and Telemetry.funnel's signature (Telemetry.luau:100) has no slot for one, so no call site could supply it. RoomService.luau:297 already mints room.sessionId ('S%d-%d') and MachineFlow logs it in warnings (:1397), but it never reaches AnalyticsService. Consequence: with one-time-funnel semantics, everything after a player's first traversal is deduped — you can learn 'this player ever finished a first round' but never 'how many matches per visit', 'did session 3 convert better than session 1', or 'how many rematches happened'. Every session by the same player is conflated by construction.
- ONE-TIME AND PER-MATCH SEMANTICS ARE MIXED IN A SINGLE FUNNEL. Step 1 (Joined) is per-server-join, and because every match runs on a freshly reserved server teleported in from the menu (dev-src PlaylistService.luau:166-169), it is effectively per-party-teleport. Steps 2/3/4 are per-match code that re-executes on every session in that server (MachineFlow.luau:1407-1410 respawns preFlow after each match). Step 5 is by definition a second-session event. All five are logged into the same nil-session funnel, so the recurring steps silently collapse to their first occurrence. The developers half-know this — MachineFlow.luau:472-473 "One-time funnel -- Analytics dedupes repeats per player" — but the funnel is named 'KenopsiaRetention' and is expected (docs/INNER-GAME-PLAN.md:248-249) to make retention gates measurable, which a deduped one-time funnel cannot do.
- STEP SKIPS PRODUCE IMPOSSIBLE CONVERSION RATES. Step 2 iterates room.members at preFlow time (MachineFlow.luau:474-477) while steps 3 and 4 iterate ctx.participantUserIds, snapshotted at countdown (RoomService.luau:298-299). A player who takes a seat after preFlow has already run gets steps 3 and 4 with no step 2, which will render as a >100% step2->step3 conversion. Conversely, solo players get step 2 and can never get step 3 (see unfinished item).
- ANALYTICS FAILURE IS ITSELF INVISIBLE. Telemetry.luau:37-41 warnOnce is keyed on two constants only — 'event' (:96) and 'funnel' (:107) — so a universe where AnalyticsService is rejecting calls emits exactly two warnings for the entire server lifetime and then goes silent, with no dropped-event counter. Combined with the pcall wrappers at :92 and :103, a totally non-functioning analytics pipeline is indistinguishable from a healthy one in the server log.
- BOOT-CRITICAL COUPLING IN THE ANALYTICS MODULE. Telemetry.luau:135 does two unbounded WaitForChild calls ("ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild(\"Kenopsia\"):WaitForChild(\"Remotes\")") on the main boot thread, at Main.server.luau:17 — before Profiles.start(), EmoteService.start(), CharacterService.start() and MachineFlow.start() (Main.server.luau:18-21), none of which are pcall-guarded. In practice RoomService.start() at :16 creates the folder synchronously (RoomService.luau:583 ensureRemotes -> :562-563), so this does not hang today; but any future reordering or partial failure of RoomService.start turns the analytics module into a total server-boot hang.

### Uncertainties

- NEEDS_RUNTIME: whether AnalyticsService is actually enabled and ingesting for universe 10640788131. Every call is pcall-wrapped (Telemetry.luau:92, :103) and failures warn once, so the code cannot tell you. Settling observation: open Creator Dashboard -> Analytics -> Funnels for universe 10640788131 and check whether a funnel named 'KenopsiaRetention' exists with a non-zero step-1 count; OR join a published MainGame server and grep the server log for '[Telemetry] LogFunnelStepEvent failed'. Absence of that warn plus a non-zero dashboard count is the only proof any of this data exists.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME: how often the step-5 early return at MachineFlow.luau:480 actually fires. This depends on whether the client shows a pressable READY control during the 'Selecting' waiting stage (RoomService.setWaitingStage(waitingToken, "Selecting") at MachineFlow.luau:468). Server-side the press is accepted (RoomService.luau:356 allows Waiting and Starting), but the GUI gate is client-side and the repo mirror of KenopsiaClient is flagged as behind live. Settling observation: run a 2-player published session, both players press READY during the 6.4 s roulette after a verdict, and check whether the server log shows the countdown starting before the 'info' packet — if it does, RematchAccepted was skipped for both.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME: the real production latency distribution of the KenopsiaProfile_v1 UpdateAsync (Profiles.luau:110), which determines how much of the bounce window 'Joined' is blind to. Settling observation: instrument-free proxy — compare Creator Analytics 'Joined' step-1 counts against Roblox's own visit/session count for placeId 110672791536316 over the same window; a persistent shortfall is the hidden early-exit cohort.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME: what fraction of touch players sit at SavedQualityLevel <= 4, i.e. how close 'MobileBlackScreen' is to a pure mobile headcount. Settling observation: on the published place, compare the MobileBlackScreen custom-event count in Creator Analytics against the mobile-device session count for the same period; a ratio near 1.0 confirms the event carries no diagnostic signal.
- Not verified: whether the live Kenopsia_DEV place (129909297895850) matches dev-src. The connected Studio session was MainGame only (weppy connection reports placeId 110672791536316; mcp__Roblox_Studio__list_roblox_studios returned an empty list). The 'menu place has zero telemetry' finding is [SOURCE] from dev-src + kenopsia-dev.project.json only. Settling observation: open 129909297895850 in Studio and grep its ServerScriptService and StarterPlayerScripts for 'Analytics' and 'Telemetry'.
- Not verified: whether the 60 s rematch window (MachineFlow.luau:1281) is ever exceeded in production. The computed normal path is ~18 s (FinalScore 6.0 + ShiftReport 5.0 + 0.4 + LobbyReveal 6.4), but cleanup() at MachineFlow.luau:1383 and cleanupToWaiting at :1405 were not traced for yields. Settling observation: a published session with a stopwatch between the final board and the post-verdict 'info' card, or a print of os.clock() - rematch.until_ at MachineFlow.luau:501.

## Disabled-trial registry, content rating (gore/blood), and the register of deliberately-unfinished/dormant work — Kenopsia MainGame (place 110672791536316) + Kenopsia_DEV (129909297895850)

Fifteen trials are declared; exactly three are playable. Schedulability is gated twice and independently — an id must appear in `GameConfig.Playlist.TrialIds` AND its `MachineFlow` registry row must declare `ready = true`. [RUNTIME] The live place has ready=true on exactly three rows (MachineFlow lines 88/100/130) and ready=false on twelve. Eleven of the twelve are byte-shaped 75-line "FRAMEWORK STUB" files that play a compliant no-op round; the twelfth (SortingFloor) is fully built and deliberately held back until after release. This is designed dormancy, not breakage: every stub carries an explicit header saying so, and the two gates carry comments explaining why listing an id cannot leak it. BREATHER is the one dormant row with a content problem: its registry subtitle is `"ONE CIGARETTE. THIRTY SECONDS. NOTHING ELSE._"` and its full design in docs/MP-04 is a cigarette-smoking simulator (hold-to-inhale, ember, lung meter, cough, ashtray) — content Roblox does not permit at any maturity level. [RUNTIME] That string is the only occurrence of "CIGARETTE" across all 115 scripts in the live place, it is on a ready=false row, and no code anywhere reads the registry's `subtitle` field, so nothing reaches a player today. On the gore side the E6 "mild ~14+" decision cut blood volume to ~40% behind one `GORE` table, but it did NOT remove body destruction: `shatter()` still hides the whole body and spawns 5 chunk parts plus 2 leg-shaped chunk parts plus a blood fountain, and it is called on the second-mine death, the compactor death, and the Canteen execution. Both docs/INNER-GAME-PLAN.md and docs/RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md state "no dismemberment"; the shipped code's own comment on that function is "ZERSTUECKELUNG: der Koerper zerreisst komplett in Brocken". The checklist's item 6 asks the user to re-answer the Creator Hub age questionnaire DOWN on the strength of that claim, which the code does not support. Separately, [RUNTIME] confirmed the repo mirror studio-src/ is behind live: `BloodFX.mark` exists live at line 97 and is absent from the mirror, while `CanteenProtocol.luau:565` calls it — a Rojo push from studio-src today would break every Canteen execution.

GameConfig.luau:49-51 'Each stays unschedulable until its registry entry flips ready = true in the Integrate stage (the second gate), so listing them here leaks nothing.' ### Risks

- BREATHER SHIPS A TOBACCO REFERENCE IN THE PLACE, AND ITS DESIGN IS A SMOKING SIMULATOR. Registry row verbatim [SOURCE MachineFlow.luau:199-206, and RUNTIME-confirmed identical in the live place]: `id = "breather",` / `displayName = "BREATHER",` / `icon = "Factory",` / `subtitle = "ONE CIGARETTE. THIRTY SECONDS. NOTHING ELSE._",` / `tagline = "INHALE. NOT TOO DEEP.",` / `ready = false,` / `showInterRoundScore = false,` / `module = Breather,`. YES it references a cigarette — explicitly, in the subtitle. The trial IS disabled, at gate 2 only: MachineFlow.luau:204 `ready = false`. Gate 1 does NOT block it — GameConfig.luau:55 lists `"breather"` in TrialIds, and Playlist.luau:33 lists it in Playlist.Ids. So a single one-word edit arms it. Roblox forbids depicting the use of tobacco/smoking products at every maturity level, so this is not a rating-dial question, it is a policy question.
- BREATHER'S DESIGN, NOT JUST ITS SUBTITLE, IS THE PROBLEM. docs/MP-04-TRIAL-DESIGNS.md:506-515: 'Four players stand at the loading-dock railing under a `Sodium` lamp. Each holds a cigarette (`Bone` stub with a `Signal` neon ember). Hold LMB to inhale: the ember brightens and the cigarette length shrinks along a server-owned burn meter; release to exhale... A LUNG meter fills while inhaling... if it tops out, the player COUGHS'. :529 'ELIMINATED = caught by supervisor... the round text is `CONFISCATED.`'. :539 'ashtray drum `Rust`'. :542 'Dynamic props. cigarettes (2 Parts each), puff Parts (client only).' If breather is ever implemented as designed it cannot ship. The already-planned mitigation is in docs/MASTERPLAN.md:154 — rename the icon 'cigarette→"breath"' — but the mechanic itself would still need replacing.
- CONTAINMENT TODAY IS REAL AND THREE-DEEP, WHICH IS WHY THIS IS A LATENT RISK RATHER THAN A LIVE ONE. (1) gate 2 ready=false; (2) [RUNTIME] the live place has zero readers of `.subtitle` across 115 scripts, and the only packets carrying trial identity to a client are `{kind="selection", ..., name = trial.displayName}` (MachineFlow.luau:474) and `{kind="info", name, icon, id}` (MachineFlow.luau:483-488) — neither carries subtitle or tagline; (3) MachineLayout.client.luau:262-267 requires TrialClients/<id> lazily, keyed on the SERVER-chosen id, so the breather stub's `controlsText` line 'INHALE. NOT TOO DEEP.' (TrialClients/breather.luau:20) is unreachable. [RUNTIME] 'CIGARETTE' occurs exactly once in the live place, at MachineFlow.luau:202. Even so, the string is shipped in a published place and is one flag from being a design commitment.
- DISMEMBERMENT EXISTS IN THE SHIPPED BUILD, CONTRADICTING BOTH PLANNING DOCUMENTS. docs/INNER-GAME-PLAN.md:295-296 'implication over gore (compactor hard-cut, execution flash + blackout, burgundy PS1 splats within shipped GoreClient caps, no dismemberment, no horror extremes)'. docs/RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md:64 'Decision E6 made the game mild (~14+): implication over gore, no dismemberment, hard-cut deaths, ~40 % blood.' But [RUNTIME, live GoreClient lines 434-448] `shatter()` still runs `gibs(pos, GORE.Shatter.gibs, false)` then `gibs(pos, GORE.Shatter.long, true)` then `fountain(pos)`, and the function's own header comment is 'ZERSTUECKELUNG: der Koerper zerreisst komplett in Brocken (Minen-/Snipertod)'. `long = true` produces leg-shaped parts: GoreClient.client.luau:273 `p.Size = Vector3.new(0.32 + math.random() * 0.1, 1.0 + math.random() * 0.6, 0.32 + math.random() * 0.1)`. Server-side, BloodFX.shatter first makes the whole body invisible (BloodFX.luau:93-97 `if d:IsA("MeshPart") ... then d.Transparency = 1`). Body-into-chunks is what is on screen for the surviving players.
- THE E6 REDUCTION REMOVED THE HALF-BODY CRAWL, NOT THE BODY DESTRUCTION — AND THE TWO WERE CONFLATED. What actually changed on 30.08 is documented in the code: GoreClient.client.luau:406-411 'KEIN GEWORFENER UNTERKOERPER MEHR. Hier stand gibs(pos, 4, true) -- vier beinfoermige Brocken, und genau die haben den Laeufer optisch halbiert. Die Mine verwundet jetzt, sie amputiert nicht'; Minefield.luau:943-949 removed `XBotLegless`. That fix applies to `maim()` only — the FIRST mine. `shatter()` was untouched and still fires `long = 2`. So 'no dismemberment' is true of the wounded-crawl state and false of every death.
- RELEASE-CHECKLIST ITEM 6 ASKS THE USER TO RE-ANSWER THE AGE QUESTIONNAIRE DOWNWARD ON A CLAIM THE CODE DOES NOT SUPPORT. docs/RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md:63-65 '**Age questionnaire — RE-AUDIT DOWN.** The 24.08 answer was "heavy fantasy gore". Decision E6 made the game mild (~14+): implication over gore, no dismemberment, hard-cut deaths, ~40 % blood. The questionnaire must be re-answered to match'. Acting on that line as written would file an inaccurate questionnaire, which is a moderation-action risk that is strictly worse than an over-strict rating. Either shatter must lose its gibs, or the questionnaire must keep the higher answer.
- WHAT A PLAYER ACTUALLY SEES TODAY, per path, from the live constants. Palette: DEEP = Color3.fromRGB(50, 4, 4), DARK = Color3.fromRGB(80, 0, 0), DRIED = Color3.fromRGB(36, 3, 3) (GoreClient.client.luau:15-17) — deep burgundy, flat SmoothPlastic, PS1 look, not photoreal red. Budget table [RUNTIME, live GoreClient 41-61]: Droplets 70 / DropletsT 24; Splats 45 / SplatsT 22; Pools 16; PoolLife 11; Mist 20; BurstBase 4; BurstPerK 6; JetRate 55; JetHold 1.0; JetDrops 5; `Kill = { k = 1.0, gibs = 3, pool = 1.8, poolVar = 0.8, sats = 1 }`; `Maim = { k = 0.8, pool = 1.1, poolVar = 0.7 }`; `Hit = { k = 0.5, pool = 0.7, poolVar = 0.5 }`; `Shatter = { k = 1.3, gibs = 5, long = 2, pool = 2.1, poolVar = 0.8, sats = 1, drops = 7 }`. Every 'war N' comment on those lines records roughly a halving from the pre-E6 values.
- BIRD HUNTING (mildest, ready=true): a sniper hit plays `BloodFX.hit` — one mist puff at k=0.5 (10 particles), ~7 droplets, one 0.7-1.2 stud pool — plus an ImpactBody sound; the runner falls and plays a death clip parked on its last keyframe. BirdHunting.luau:1170-1171 and :1165-1167 'the runner FALLS where they stand -- Humanoid.Health = 0 lets PS1Animate play the field death and park it on the last keyframe; no corpse clone, no fling. One localized blood hit'. No gibs, no shatter anywhere in BirdHunting.
- DEAD ZONE, first mine (ready=true): `BloodFX.maim` — mist k=0.8, one 1.1-1.8 stud pool, two sounds, then a blood drip splat every 1.6-2.4 s while the player crawls on a WHOLE body (BloodFX.luau:22-23 BLEED_MIN 1.6 / BLEED_VAR 0.8, 'war 1.0 / 0.6'; :148-153; Minefield.luau:960). Screen: gorefx power 0.8 (Minefield.luau:884). Feed line 'WOUNDED. CRAWL.' (Minefield.luau:968).
- DEAD ZONE, second mine and compactor catch (ready=true): body made invisible, then 5 irregular chunks + 2 leg-shaped chunks that ragdoll with velocity (GoreClient.client.luau:283-284) and leave their own splat where they land (:286-288), a blood jet at Rate 55 for 1.0 s, five pools (one 2.1-2.9 stud + one satellite), 7 extra droplets, two sounds. Screen: gorefx power 0.8 / 1.0. The victim's own screen is covered from second 0 by a death card ('YOU STEPPED WRONG. TWICE.' Minefield.luau:904; 'PROCESSED.' Minefield.luau:1423) — Minefield.luau:1418-1421 'KEINE Crush-Nahaufnahme existiert... die Kamera des Opfers sieht nie ein Zerquetschen.' The SURVIVORS have no such card and watch the whole thing.
- CANTEEN PROTOCOL, execution (ready=true): a boss NPC shoots a seated, unarmed player-avatar in the head. First a localized hit at the head, then 0.15 s later the diner model is destroyed and the full shatter package fires, plus a blood mark that persists to round end (`BloodFX.mark(at, 2.6, ...)`, live BloodFX line 97 `ev({ t = "pool", ..., keep = true })`). Screen: gorefx power 0.9. Death card 'PROTOCOL VIOLATION - <name>' and a feed line to everyone else (CanteenProtocol.luau:466-472). This is the most explicitly execution-framed content in the game and it is live.
- GENERIC DEATH FOR EVERY OTHER TRIAL (the path the twelve dormant trials would use): TrialKit Round:kill -> `BloodFX.kill` = 3 chunks + two pools + mist k=1.0, gorefx power 0.9, death card 'TERMINATED.' (TrialKit.luau:742-748). So arming any dormant trial that calls Round:kill immediately adds gibs to that trial.
- LENS BLOOD IS THE LOUDEST CUE AND IS ACKNOWLEDGED AS SUCH. KenopsiaClient.client.luau:2526-2531 'Blut auf der LINSE ist der lauteste Gore-Reiz, den das Spiel hat -- es klebt im Bild statt in der Welt und fuellt auf dem Handy den halben Schirm.' Current budget (:2541-2552): `Veil = 0.72` (war 0.62), `VeilGain = 0.26`, `FadeScale = 0.65`, `Blobs = 3` (war 5), `BlobsGain = 5` (war 9), `BlobSize = 14`, `BlobGrow = 55`, `BlobLife = 0.8`, `BlobVar = 1.0`, `Gate = 0.28`. Net effect: a full-screen red wash at 28-46 % opacity plus 3-8 falling red blobs, gone in ~0.36 s, suppressed past ~31.6 studs.
- OTHER DORMANT CONTENT THAT WOULD CLASH WITH A MILD/14+ TARGET IF ARMED. CARRIER (ready=false): a syringe is the weapon — docs/MP-04-TRIAL-DESIGNS.md:444 'the Carrier infects a Clean player by getting within 3 studs and pressing LMB'; :455 'LMB `stab` (Carrier only)'; :482 'Dynamic props. syringe (2 Parts)'; :485 'infect = `ImpactBody` + `Blood.Blood1` at low volume'. Registry text MachineFlow.luau:192-193 `subtitle = "ONE OF YOU IS ALREADY SICK._"` / `tagline = "FIND THE NEEDLE. OR AVOID IT."`. A needle used as a weapon on other players reads harder than 'mild'. CRAWLER (ready=false) MachineFlow.luau:222 `subtitle = "IT WANTS A BACK TO BREAK._"`. SORTING FLOOR (built, ready=false) MachineFlow.luau:252-253 `subtitle = "GOOD LEFT. BAD RIGHT. NO EXCEPTIONS._"` / `tagline = "MISFILE AND YOU FOLLOW IT."` — three strikes opens the station floor and drops the player toward an incinerator maw (SortingFloor.luau:3-4, :33-34 'floor, belt + frame, four stations (pad = trapdoor, pit below...), rule board + frame, incinerator maw').
- TONE, NOT GORE: the Machine's narration is clinical-dystopian about human bodies. MachineVoice.luau:73 '"ONE SUBJECT PROCESSED. THE LINE MOVES ON."', :75 '"THE FIRST BODY IS LOGGED. MORE WILL FOLLOW."', :76 '"ONE LESS SUBJECT. OUTPUT UNAFFECTED."', :77 '"INITIAL CASUALTY RECORDED. PROCEED."'. No profanity, no slurs, no sexual content. Fine at 13+/14+; would block a 9+/all-ages rating.
- NO GAMBLING OR PAY-FOR-POWER SURFACE TODAY, AND THAT WAS DELIBERATE. docs/INNER-GAME-PLAN.md:269-271 'Zero pay-for-power. Crate keys are **earned only** (quests/streaks), never sold in MainGame; odds listed in DEV''s crate ceremony — mild-maturity / no-gambling-optics safe.' ProgressionRules.luau:313 `fragments = #completed * 1, -- one fragment per completed quest`. All gamepass ids are 0 and PrivateServerPrice is 0, so nothing is purchasable at all.
- OPERATIONAL RISK, HIGH SEVERITY: a Rojo push from studio-src/ today deletes `BloodFX.mark` (live line 97) while `CanteenProtocol.luau:565` still calls it. The call sits in an un-pcall'd `task.delay(SHATTER_BEAT, ...)` body (CanteenProtocol.luau:540), so the error would also swallow the gorefx announce at :579 for every Canteen execution. RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md:68-73 already forbids the push ('DO THIS BEFORE ANY ROJO CONNECT'), but does not name this consequence.

### Uncertainties

- NEEDS_RUNTIME — nobody has watched a death. Every statement above about 'how much blood a player sees' is derived from constants, not from pixels. RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md:95-97 defines the exact observation that would settle it: 'first mine → wounded crawl on a WHOLE body with the normal clip, no thrown lower half, trail still dripping; second mine → death; blood visibly lighter on a phone; no lens blood past ~32 studs'. Settling observation: one 2-player DEAD ZONE round with a screen capture at the moment of a second-mine death, from a SURVIVOR's camera (not the victim's — the victim is behind a death card from second 0). I could not run it: [RUNTIME] `list_roblox_studios` returned `{"studios":[]}`, matching RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md:86-88's report that the official Studio MCP has been dead all session.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME — GUI TEXT WAS NOT EXHAUSTIVELY SWEPT FOR TOBACCO WORDING. I confirmed [RUNTIME] that 'CIGARETTE' appears in no script in the live place except MachineFlow:202, and that IconPool holds only 8 non-tobacco glyphs. I did NOT enumerate every TextLabel/TextButton `Text` property in game.StarterGui, and the KNOWN HAZARD list says KenopsiaClient and the GUI are exactly where the mirror is behind. Settling observation: `search_property` (PRO) over game.StarterGui for Text values containing CIGARETTE / SMOKE / INHALE, plus a look at game.ServerStorage.KenopsiaUI_Backup_20260828_191906 which [RUNTIME] also carries a TrialClients.breather copy.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME — whether an animation clip for smoking exists or was published. docs/MP-04-TRIAL-DESIGNS.md:520-521 says 'a bespoke smoke pose is an AnimationIds placeholder' and :923 lists `Smoke` (breather) among clips that 'would be used if published later'. If a Smoke clip was actually published under the group (832614570), that is a shipped tobacco asset independent of the ready flag. Settling observation: read AnimationIds.luau in the LIVE place (the mirror is listed as behind for AnimationIds) and check for a `Smoke` key with a non-placeholder id.
- NEEDS_RUNTIME — the live KenopsiaClient's screen-blood budget. I read the `G` table at studio-src KenopsiaClient.client.luau:2541-2552, but that file is the single worst mirror offender (RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md:69 'KenopsiaClient by ~750 lines'). The GORE and BloodFX halves I did verify live and they matched; the lens-blood half I did not. Settling observation: read game.StarterPlayer.StarterPlayerScripts.KenopsiaClient around the GORE_SCREEN block and compare Veil/VeilGain/Blobs/Gate.
- UNRESOLVED, NOT RUNTIME — what Roblox's questionnaire actually scores this as. I can state what the code renders; I cannot state which maturity band Roblox assigns to 'humanoid body replaced by seven burgundy chunks plus a blood jet'. My reading is that it is above 'mild' and that RELEASE-CHECKLIST item 6 should not be actioned as written, but the authoritative answer comes from the Creator Hub questionnaire itself, not from me.
- CONTRADICTION IN THE DOCS, worth flagging rather than resolving. docs/INNER-GAME-PLAN.md:67 says 'GoreClient keeps its shipped touch caps (48/40)' — those are the PRE-E6 values; the live table is DropletsT 24 / SplatsT 22. Likewise CanteenProtocol.luau:531 describes shatter as 'burst 2.2, 14 Brocken' and :551-557 reasons about 'POOL_LIFE ist 18 s' and 'MAX_POOLS = 30', all pre-E6 numbers (live: k 1.3, 7 chunks, PoolLife 11, Pools 16). The comments are stale, the code is current. No functional consequence, but anyone reading those comments to judge the rating would get the wrong figures.
- dev-src DIVERGENCE, low severity, noted for completeness. Kenopsia_DEV's GameConfig declares `TrialIds = { "canteen_protocol", "quality_control", "target_calibration" }` (dev-src/.../GameConfig.luau:31) — three ids that exist in neither Playlist.Ids nor the MainGame registry, served by three self-contained modules with no gore. The two places share a module NAME and not a schema (DEV has no PerSession, and adds a MemoryStore block). This is two independent games in one repo, not a broken reference; I did not verify it against the live DEV place (place 129909297895850 was not the connected Studio instance — [RUNTIME] the connected client reported placeId 110672791536316).
